## Atlanta

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*  
*kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroḥ cṛī-caraṇāravindam*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most holy Vyāsa-pūjā day, please accept our humble, prostrated obeisance in the dust of the tracks made by your lotus feet as you traverse the globe on your mercy mission. Those with faulty vision will not see that you are yet the most glorified, swanlike, eternally liberated wandering mendicant.

Those with clear vision can understand the very principle of the spiritual master and thus cannot mistake your appearance or disappearance to be that of simply another mundane personality, or even a great personality. From your mouth issue the same Vedic truths, purely spoken, as issued from the very lips of Śrī Kṛṣṇa upon His instructions of enlightenment to the universal creator, Brahmā, from whom in turn has descended the preceptorial line in whose midst you have appeared. Thus Śrī Kṛṣṇa states,

*ācāryaṁ māṁ vijanīyān*  
*nāvamanyeta karhicit*  
*na martya-buddhyāsūyeta*  
*sarva-deva-mayo guruḥ*

**”One should understand the spiritual master to be as good as I am. No one should be jealous of his as an ordinary man, because the spiritual master is the sum total of all demigods.” (*****Bhāg.*** **11.17.27)**

Envy of the Supreme Personality of Godhead is the root cause of the illusion of suffering in perpetual forgetfulness of the Lord and His devotional service. Devotional service is factually our eternal, blissful life of perfect knowledge.

Having willfully transgressed against the principle of pure love of God in that blissful life of variegated service to the Supreme Lord, we have doomed ourselves to the agonizing gulag of His material creation. Dare we fallen souls mistake your identity as other than the universal teacher, our deliverer?

Therefore your proposal of surrender to the Lord in love and service is for the good of all the living entities of the world, regardless of their form of bodily entrapment, regardless of their temporary allegiances in terms of body, mind, or doctrine. No one should mistake this precept to be similarly sectarian – that it is good only for some certain section of people. Your teaching is properly accepted as the panacea for the suffering of every living entity in the material universe because it revives eternal loving service to Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, and thus fulfills every auspicious end of life.

You are eternally in your *vāṇī* in the teachings that you have transmitted unto us, in the further distribution of your books that preserve and protect those teachings, and in the International Society for Krishna Consciousness and its bona fide activities, which form the web of devotional association necessary and sufficient for anyone to uplift himself by those teachings.

Śrī Kṛṣṇa inquired of Arjuna after delivering to him the entire *Bhagavad-gītā,*

*kaccid etac chrutaṁ pārtha*  
*tvayaikāgreṇa cetasā*  
*kaccid ajñāna-sammohaḥ*  
*pranaṣṭas te dhanañjaya*

**”O conqueror of wealth, Arjuna, have you heard this attentively with your mind? And are your illusions and ignorance now dispelled?” (Bg. 18.72)**

Arjuna replied to the Lord,

*naṣṭo mohaḥ smṛtir labdhā*  
*tvat-prasādān mayācyuta*  
*sthito ‘smi gata-sandehaḥ*  
*kariṣye vacanaṁ tava*

**”My dear Kṛṣṇa, O infallible one, my illusion is now gone. I have regained my memory by Your mercy, and I am now firm and free from doubt and am prepared to act according to Your instructions.” (Bg. 18.73)**

The mercy of the Lord is absolute. Arjuna accepted the Lord’s mercy through his hearing process with all attention and with his full senses, mind, and understanding. We must be similarly prepared to hear from you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and surrender accordingly with full understanding and conviction.

*yasya deve parā bhaktir*  
*yathā deve tathā gurau*  
*tasyaite kathitā hy arthā*  
*prakācānte mahātmanaḥ*

**”Only unto those great souls who simultaneously have implicit faith in both the Lord and the spiritual master are all the imports of Vedic knowledge automatically revealed.”**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have defined faith as “implicit trust in something sublime.” Kṛṣṇa Himself is the Supreme Great, the greatest of all, and what He has spoken or has caused to be spoken is being delivered by you. As the Lord is nondifferent from His teachings, so His teachings as delivered by you are also nondifferent from the Lord. Let each of us, then, have full faith in your instructions and agree to hear them submissively and carry them out, each of us in his or her own life.

Surely if we reflect even for a moment, we will see that, since even a partial application of your mercy has relieved us of a volume of nescience that could not be dispelled by hundreds of ritualistic sacrifices performed by fully qualified *brāhmaṇas,* so how much more benefit will accrue to us if we just agree to surrender fully, based on the same understanding as Arjuna when he heard personally from the Lord.

Śrīla Prabhupāda! Had you not been as determined as Arjuna and as certain of all help from the Lord by the mercy of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Prabhupāda, would we today be privileged to offer this homage? Therefore, O lord and master, please hear our prayer:

Please allow us to recognize our absolute need to loyalty discharge our absolute duty to you on the absolute plane. Such faithful service, combined with your ever well-wishes, can deliver each of us, along with the entire world, from our current suffering.

Let us remember you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, a t every step of our lives. Let us remember you in every one of our dealings among ourselves, and let us remember you in our dealings with the innocent nondevotees. Let us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, remember you when we seek to destroy the nescience of forgetfulness of Kṛṣṇa that is rampant in the world.

Had we knowledge, we would not suffer under the enslavement of the bodily misconception. Had we courage, we would not fear the ordeal of purification. Had we love for you, we would not hesitate.

Let us, having been awakened by your forceful delivery of the message of Godhead, now follow you in taking up the determination of Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī Prabhupāda, who has established within this material world the mission to fulfill the desire of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu to deliver all the fallen souls. This righteous duty is justly assumed by those enlightened with transcendental knowledge in the *rūpānuga* line.

Therefore, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us fully adopt the ecstatic devotional life as instructed by Lord Caitanya Himself to Śrīla Rūpa and Sanātana Gosvāmīs and as given to us by you, both by example and precept, for our deliverance as well as our transcendental pleasure. Let us not accept anything on our own behalf, but let us be intelligent in how to not only accept but to arrange for all kinds of opportunities for preaching the glories of the Lord.

Let each of us prevail upon one another with a straw in his teeth to abide by your transcendental order in all matters, and let those of us thus prevailed upon take a straw in our teeth, falling down before the mercy of the Lord, made manifest in the assembly of Vaiṣṇavas. Let us be the love-of-God-smitten society that you envision.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, had you not appeared, our fate would have been most uncertain. Certainly we, like the countless other helpless living entities trapped in this material world, would have remained so, eternally bound by our lust, anger, and greed, without hope of release. By the mercy of the Supreme Lord, who is ever seated in our hearts, you have come to us even now, and now we are going back to Godhead.

ei rūpe brahmāṇḍa bhramite koṇa bhāgyavān jīva  
guru-kṛṣṇa-prasāde pāya bhakti-latā-bīja

Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us at this moment determine to adopt the same resolve as Arjuna had, depending fully on the mercy of the Lord through the medium of His divine instructions, as you have delivered them to us. Let us determine not simply to bathe in the dust raised by your wide traveling and preaching, but to also follow in those footsteps.

Thus we are inspired to remember you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and to offer this Vyāsa-pūjā homage to you in great joy and celebration. How many times shall we see this most blessed event in our short lives?

*Your humble servants in Atlanta,*

## Auckland

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisance in the dust of your lotus feet. By your mercy only are we still engaged in your transcendental service.

The Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement, which you began single-handedly in the Western countries, continues to grow and give shelter to sincere persons interested in spiritual life. Your compassionate nature, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is the wonderful example for all generations of devotees to try to follow, although no one will ever compare to Your Divine Grace.

You are the special personality empowered by Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu to spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement to every town and village. You had unflinching faith in the prediction of the Lord that one day this would happen. Other followers of your Guru Mahārāja doubted this prediction, thinking it just an interesting idea, but they did not believe it to be possible. You made it a reality, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and we, your disciples and grand disciples, are eternally grateful to you.

As the materialistic society becomes madder by the moment, we cling to your instructions as our only shelter. Thank you for taking so much trouble to translate the cream of the Vedic literatures, *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and adding your brilliant purports. And for the thousands of other things you did, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we thank you. A disciple can never repay his debt to his spiritual master but must nonetheless try. With whatever strength we have, we pray to you to please allow us to continue in your service, birth after birth.

*Your servants in Auckland, New Zealand,*

## Baltimore

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisance, and also our apologies for not having enough faith in your instructions. Being so neophyte, we have been neglecting to preach as you have shown us.

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ*  
*paṅuṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande*  
*śrī-guruṁ dīna-taraṇam*

**”By the grace of the** ***guru*** **a lame man climb mountains, a blind man can see, and one who is mute can speak eloquent poetry.”**

In a letter to a disciple, you wrote that in India it is the duty of all those who work with the railway to make sure that the train’s wheels keep moving, and in that way the authorities can judge if the train lines are functioning nicely. In the same way, you said, it is the duty of all the member of our Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement to make sure that your books are printed and distributed, and in that way we can judge whether or not the preaching is going on nicely. You emphasized that the books should not just be printed and stored in some warehouse but that they should be distributed, and in this way the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement would spread.

So, twelve years have passed since your physical body has left us, and so much has happened that could have derailed the train. But you have stated that as long as the disciples follow the instructions of the spiritual master, staying on track like the cars of a railway, you would push them back to Godhead.

On this most auspicious day of your appearance in this world, we here in New Kulīnagrāma pray to you that although we are lame, blind, and mute, we can keep the train of your preaching mission on track.

Thank you for coming here and staying with us in your instructions, paraphernalia, and pastimes. We are attempting to distribute your books, *Back to Godhead, Bhagavad-gītā,* etc., as well as take care of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurasundara and Lord Jagannātha, who are very dear to you, and to improve our *gurukula* and prasādam distribution. Thank you for being our ever well-wisher and friend. Please allow us to always please you and be your ever well-wishers.

*Your servants in Baltimore.*

## Barcelona

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisance at your divine lotus feet. All glories unto Your Divine Grace.

We feel very happy to be under the shade of your lotus feet. Although sometimes our minds are not fixed in your service due to our lack of advancement in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, whenever we remember Your Divine Grace and your divine instructions, we feel relief from the pangs of material existence and gain strength to push on Lord Caitanya’s mission. That is your mercy.

Although you never came physically to Barcelona, it is a fact that you are present here. Otherwise, how is it possible that gradually the people of Barcelona are receiving the mercy of Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda? By the mercy of the pure devotee of the Lord, even the most fallen souls can receive the direct glance of the Lord. Obviously, we are not pure devotees of the Lord, because very often we forget that the goal of life is to develop *prema-bhakti*, pure love of Godhead. For this reason we cannot by ourselves deliver devotional service to anyone. But because you are very kind and merciful toward those who suffer, many auspicious and transcendental things are happening in a materialistic city like Barcelona.

By your mercy, Lord Caitanya and Lord Nityānanda have personally come to Barcelona in Their Deity forms.

By your mercy, your books are regularly reaching the hands of the conditioned souls here in Barcelona.

By your mercy, the holy name of the Lord is spreading all over the main streets of Barcelona three days a week.

By your mercy, you have come personally in the form of your *mūrti* so that we can become purified by doing personal service unto you.

By your mercy, the people of Barcelona have a place where they can go whenever they want to associate with the Lord and His devotees.

By your mercy, many plates of *kṛṣṇa-prasādam* will be distributed very soon to the Catalan people.

By your mercy, very soon the people of Barcelona will see the smiling face of Lord Jagannātha during His Ratha-yātrā.

Whatever transcendental events happened in the past, are happening in the present, or will happen in the future here in Barcelona are due only to your mercy. Of course, your mercy is reaching all the corners of the world, but because we are very small, we try to speak about your glories mainly here in Barcelona.

Please give us the strength to continue serving your mission; please empower us so that we can serve as useful instruments of Your Divine Grace for the pleasure of Lord Caitanya and for the benefit of the conditioned souls.

*Your servants at ISKCON Barcelona, Spain,*

## Baroda

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisance at your lotus feet. All glories to you on your Vyāsa-pūjā day!

We think it is not possible to even begin to describe the ocean of your glories. You astounded the world by establishing this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in every corner of the globe. You were the only preacher bold enough to go everywhere to deliver the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to us wretched fallen souls, who were burning in the forest fire of material illusion, whose flames are being continuously fanned by the influence of Kali-yuga.

Still, you have left us with the difficult task of continuing to push on this movement the world over. The challenge is great: to defeat the forces of materialism and impersonalism. We feel very encouraged by the progress Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is making in such places as the Soviet Union, China, Brazil, and Australia. Devotees from these places and from around the world gather each year at Māyāpur and enliven and encourage one another to unceasingly carry forward the mission of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. But despite this, we see that the materialistic consciousness of the masses of people continues. Here in Bhārata-varṣa, the land of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Lord Kṛṣṇa, we sometimes feel discouraged when we see the pace at which people are adopting materialism, which is so rampant in the Western countries.

We pray to you on this auspicious day to kindly bless us with the compassion and forbearance to carry on with your mission. We pray that you may send us more and more help in the form of devotees, so that our preaching becomes more effective. We think that this is possible only by your grace.

*mūkaṁ karoti vācālaṁ*  
*paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande*  
*śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

Repeatedly begging for your mercy,

*Your servants of ISKCON Baroda, India,*

**Disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Vasu-ghoṣa daṣa

**Grand disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Berkeley

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer our obeisance unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you are Jagat-guru, the origin of the sentiment of *kṛṣṇa-prema* in the Western world and founder*-ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. Because you are always glorifying Kṛṣṇa, you dissipate the ignorance that causes us to think we are these bodies and help us to reestablish our eternal relationship with God.

Out of compassion and duty, you came here to plant the seed of devotional service. Your sacrifice and dedication to fulfill the desire of your spiritual master are glorified throughout the three worlds. For those of us endeavoring in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, these glories are beyond our full understanding and appreciation. We really know only how you have affected and changed our lives, saving us from the bewildering and miserable existence of material life, giving us happiness when we had hope of none. We offer our humble obeisance unto you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, O most dear and intimate servant of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Mahārāja, you are the destroyer of the bad qualities of this age of Kali and the protector of your disciples. Your kindness is always available. You are giving us Kṛṣṇa, revealing Kṛṣṇa as he really is. We offer our most grateful, prostrated obeisance at your lotus feet.

We offer our respectful obeisance unto you, whose complexion is a beautiful golden color, whose glance heals every soul you look upon, and who are always decorated with the loving offerings of your devotees. We pray never to leave your service.

Once a devotee asked you, “What is the meaning of ‘spiritual master?’”

”Actually, I am not your spiritual master,” you replied. “That title is simply a formality. You should think of me as your spiritual father, your eternal father.”

Just as a son and daughter can never forget a loving father, how can we ever forget your kindness, compassion, and instruction? We know you as our eternal spiritual father; others know you as their most worshipable spiritual grand father; others know you as their most respected spiritual guide and teacher; others as their only protector and friend; others as a pure devotee who gives and exemplifies the highest position of loving exchange with God; and others as the supreme welfare worker and *ācārya* for this age. However we may love and worship you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are all of these, and we offer our respectful obeisance at your lotus feet again and again.

You have asked us all to become spiritual masters and help those suffering in this material world by giving them Kṛṣṇa. Our only duty is to be an instrument of your mercy. What is that mercy? Your mercy is pure association, pure desire, pure knowledge, pure living, pure feelings, pure intelligence and expertise to preach and, most of all, pure love. These are the things you have given us – are always giving us. We simply have to give others what you are giving us. We acknowledge you eternally as our source of strength and enlightenment. May we always represent your compassion and teachings to the best of our ability. All credit and glory to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

You yourself exemplify the relationship between *guru* and disciple. You wrote, “From my point of view, I think that I am so sinful that I cannot even approach Kṛṣṇa to show me any favor. I have only one hope: my spiritual master – He is very kind. Some way or other He is dragging me towards Kṛṣṇa. That is the only hope. Otherwise, if we study our own qualification, there is none – rather I have so many disqualifications.” (letter, 2-10-69). “Whatever is being done, it is due His Divine Grace only. My business is just to carry out His order.” (letter, 3-26-69)

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the perfect teacher, always showing by example. We beg to follow humbly and please you with our service.

Performing *sādhana-bhakti* is difficult, but you are always there to encourage us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. To think that you are forever separated from us is too unbearable to contemplate. You wrote to a devotee, “Because you are a sincere servant of Kṛṣṇa and following my directions sincerely, so you are always with me.” (letter, 6-19-68). We take comfort by reading your books and letters, by listening to your lecture tapes, by associating with your sincere disciples and followers, and by engaging in service. In this way our painful feelings of separation are mitigated, and we feel happy and enlivened in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Thank you.

We offer our most humble, respectful, and grateful obeisance eternally at your lotus feet. We belong to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your devotees at New Jagannātha Purī (Berkeley, California).*

## Bhaktivedanta Manor

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
*How can we build up our book distribution, increasing it unlimitedly...?*  
*... only by your mercy.*  
*How can we expand the fame of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and offer shelter to all fallen souls...?*  
*... only by your mercy.*  
*How is it possible that we can organize society along Kṛṣṇa conscious guidelines...?*  
*... only by your mercy.*  
*How can we unite all the devotees to dedicate their energy in a cooperative spirit for preaching...?*  
*... only by your mercy.*  
*How can we make the temple a more and more wonderful haven of Kṛṣṇa consciousness...?*  
*... only by your mercy.*  
*How can we possibly build a new temple for Rādhā-Gokulānanda...?*  
*... only by your mercy.*  
*How can we develop all the projects you envisioned...?*  
*... only by your mercy.*

We feel ourselves legging so far behind in fulfilling you vision of a world field with Kṛṣṇa consciousness. So far behind, it is bewildering to us. Even to do the simplest things, even to maintain what you painstakingly set up for us, is difficult.

On this day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, please allow us the realization that your vision is not to be achieved by our personal strength or abilities. But everything will be achieved by receiving your mercy.

Nothing that you desired is impossible. Rather, because you desired it, it is inevitable.

In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* you quote your spiritual master’s words from his *Anubhāṣya*: “A person who has attracted the attention of the spiritual master by his sincere service likes to dance and chant with similarly developed Kṛṣṇa conscious devotees. The spiritual master authorizes such a devotee to deliver fallen souls in all parts of the world.”

Our taste for chanting the holy names is a reward from you. Being pleased with sincere service, you shower your mercy on the devotees, allowing them to relish transcendental pleasure in the *saṅkīrtana* movement. But no devotee may feel himself so elevated and advanced personally that he has the capability and potency to preach and deliver others. It is only when the spiritual master authorizes and empowers the disciple that love of Godhead may be distributed.

We therefore pray that we may always remember this transcendental principle. We should never be puffed up when anything goes right, and we should never be downcast that Kṛṣṇa consciousness will not be successful. One devotee remarked, “How will the B-team succeed when the A-team could not?” This is a sad misconception. There is no A-team or B-team in terms of ability and intelligence. There are simply those who have heard your desires and who are offering themselves in your service to help those desires unfold into realities.

Some may commit themselves more or less to this service. Those who commit themselves wholly and solely will receive your full mercy, their taste for chanting and dancing will increase, their eagerness for service and preaching will become more intense, and you will deliver them back to Godhead without any doubt.

We hope that we can follow in the footsteps of such devotees and become more and more dedicated to fulfilling all your desires for the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

We beg to remain your unworthy yet eternal servants,

*The devotees at Bhaktivedanta Manor, U.K.*

## Bhayandar

Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisance at your lotus feet. And please accept this poetic offering on your glorious appearance day.

My lord appeared from the spiritual sky (Vaikuṇṭha)  
as a perfect son of Ṭhākura Bhaktisiddhānta.

Ṭhākura Bhaktisiddhānta said,  
”Go and spread Lord Caitanya’s message.”

To fulfill the order of his spiritual father,  
my lord went to the Western world

On a chariot by the name of Jaladuta.  
Not an ordinary chariot, it was a puṣpa-ratha.

Across the separation of the Atlantic Ocean  
my lord came from Vṛndāvana to the city of Boston.

There was no separation – only the pastime of my lord  
acting as a pure devotee in this material world.

As a pure devotee, my lord bestowed only benedictions,  
so that all might follow Kṛṣṇa’s principles and instructions.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, our lord and master,  
give us the knowledge to stay in ISKCON forever.

We beg to remain your eternal servants.

*Your devotees at Bhayandar, India,*

Jagaj-jīvana dāsa / Guḍākeśa dāsa / Gaura Nārāyaṇa dāsa / Upajīvana dāsa / Kumbha dāsa / Rāsa-ramaṇa dāsa / Garuḍa-dhvaja dāsa / Aja dāsa / Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Kaṁsa-niṇūdana dāsa / Maheśvara dāsa / Bhakta Haridas / Bhakta Durgesh / Bhakta Pramod / Bhakta Jasmin / Bhakta Devashish / Bhakta Laab Bhai / Bhakta Laxman Bhai / Bhakta Manish / Bhaktin Kanthabain / Bhaktin Chetna

## Bhubaneswar

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

At the dust of your divine lotus feet, kindly accept the respectful obeisance of the lowest of low fallen souls – ourselves. Together with the devotees and followers of the revolutionary spiritual movement of ISKCON around the world, we beg your causeless mercy. In that way only can we once again offer our prostrated *daṇḍavat-praṇāmas* on behalf of this small but very affectionately last-founded ISKCON center of yours, on this holy day of your divine appearance.

The universally well known great temple of Lord Jagannātha has long been considered and unanimously as the center and the focal point for spreading the message and gospel of *mahā-bhāgavata-dharma* and the supreme love of Godhead. But painfully, your non-Indian devotees are still deprived of the chance to enter into the temple of Lord Jagannātha, Lord Baladeva, and Subhadrā Māi. Being puffed up with their so-called superior birth, the so-called servitors of Lord Jagannātha do not hesitate to humiliate many foreign devotees who had the good fortune of receiving your benedictions. Only the great chariot festival of the Supreme Lord Jagannātha provides the opportunity for such dear devotees of yours to get the divine *darśana* of the Lord as He sits on His great chariot of *nandī-ghoṣa* once a year. Although it was at this very sacred *dhāma* of Śrī Jagannātha Purī that Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, remaining for over eighteen years, taught how to purchase the Lord by the strength of offensless, pure chanting of the holy name – despite this, the so-called guardians of holy *dhāma* of Śrī-kṣetra still force the foreign devotees out of the temple, preventing them from having the *darśana* of the Supreme Lord in His holy abode.

This caused great pain in the innermost core of your divine heart. Thus your gracious, noble heart was overfilled with shock and compassion. The Lord has, however, understood the feelings of the heart of His pure devotee. As such, Providence made a divine arrangement for the foundation stone of a great temple of Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma to be laid by your divine hand on the 2ndof February, 1977, the all-auspicious day of the holy appearance of Lord Śrī Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu.

Thus the holy wish of the pure devotee was immediately turned to action. The structure of bricks, small iron rods, and cement, etc., is now a little more than one-quarter finished. The holy temple of Their Lordships has withstood a lot of cyclones, thunderstorms, tornadoes, scorching hot rays of the sun, thunderbolts, and dreadful rains, and yet the temple is still patiently waiting for that great day when she can welcome Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma, Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā Māi, and Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi onto her long-aspiring lap. Also, a great number of foreign devotees are waiting for the chance to have the *darśana* of the sacred Śrī Vigrahas and thus bringing the Lord’s supreme features into their eyes, minds, and hearts.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, here you once publicly declared that this temple will one day become one of the most unique temples of the world. That holy prediction of yours ensures that the project is going to be a great success. Though it is less than half constructed, the temple even now attracts devotees from every nook and corner of the world. Though the state is poor, and its inhabitants are poorer, the confidence of your fallen servants here in the success of the temple is strong, and this is due only to your causeless mercy. As a father naturally has a special loving tendency towards his youngest son, so you have bestowed your special grace upon this last-founded holy project of yours. That alone is our source of hope and confidence. We are anxiously waiting for that great day when we will see the long-cherished implementation of your divine dream – the day when the transcendental sound of the bell, conch shell, and congregational *saṅkīrtana* of jubilant devotees in this temple will vibrate around the world and attract the attention of the entire globe.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please shower your divine mercy upon us. Only by your mercy will your dream be successfully implemented. Weak, fallen, and low as we are, we did not even know how to properly serve the servant of your servitors. To become the servitor of the servitor of the servitors of Your Divine Grace will be our greatest success and supreme joy in life. Once again we offer our heartfelt obeisance at your divine lotus feet. We anxiously await that great day: the grand opening of your Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Temple in Bhubaneswar.

You*r servants, the devotees at the Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Temple in Bhubaneswar, India.*

## Bombay

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisance at your divine, glorious, lotus feet. Simply by seeing, simply by hearing about, simply by touching, simply by serving your lotus feet, the whole world is saved from the most fearful calamities.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you made Bombay, the “Gateway to India,” your transcendental office. Though you engaged your senior disciples in managing your centers around the world, you personally took charge of your office.

We are constantly remembering how you expertly managed your office – your transcendental signature, bold and confident; your own special safe-keeping system for keeping documents secure; your bank transactions with triplicate copies of all letters; how you personally negotiated with the lawyers in purchasing Hare Krishna Land for your office; and so many other phases of your office.

Here in your office we are reminded how you conducted your business of preaching to the industrialists, the scientists, the politicians, and the professionals, and how you satisfied everyone from all walks of life with your pure message.

From your office you wrote hundreds of letters to your disciples around the world, helping them to manage and encouraging them. It was here that you finished translating your *Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* and we celebrated with an ecstatic feast. In your office you established a branch of the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust to publish your transcendental literatures in all the Indian languages.

Your business being preaching, your office must reflect that mood, and we inmates of Hare Krishna Land, being your office staff, are humbly trying to serve your office. But just as the small son visits his father at the office, we also cannot do anything without your sanction, your guidance, and your help.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we humbly beg your mercy so that we may have the intelligence and spiritual strength to understand your vision and to conduct your business as you wanted it, to spread your message and the glories of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rasabihārījī, to distribute your transcendental books by the millions, and to expand your office activities unlimitedly, for your divine pleasure.

*Your humble servants at ISKCON Bombay, Hare Krishna Land, Juhu.*

## Boston

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We were born in the darkness of ignorance, blinded by the cataracts of *avidyā*, nescience. Most of us, completely unaware of our disease, only exacerbated the symptoms by embracing the path of fruitive work. Toiling hard day and night, we tirelessly endeavored to satisfy ourselves with sensual gratification. The result: our feverish material desires burned with even greater intensity within our unfulfilled hearts.

Some of us, realizing the futility of seeking happiness through gross bodily pleasures, took the way of knowledge. There we hovered on the mental plane, reading the works of the great thinkers of the world. Our initial glimmers of hope, however, ended in the gloomy despair of endless speculations and contradictions.

A few of us even dabbled in *yoga* and meditation, only to be disappointed when peace and tranquility (what to speak of becoming God in six months) were not to be had by such practices. In that afflicted condition we lost all hopes for enlightenment and resigned ourselves as helpless passengers on the wheel of repeated birth and death.

Then, as if by some miracle, our eyes were forced open by the ointment of spiritual knowledge found in your phenomenal books. Soon we realized that you alone were the torchbearer of the ultimate wisdom meant to illuminate this dark age of Kali. Through your radiant Bhaktivedanta purports, the nature of our diseased state became evident, and we placed ourselves under your expert care for treatment.

As the transcendental physician coming in the line of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, you are administering the medicine for Kali-yuga prescribed by the Supreme Lord Himself:

*harer nāma harer nāma*  
*hare nāmaiva kevalam*  
*kalau nāsty eva nāsty eva*  
*nāsty eva gatir anyathā*

**”In this age of quarrel and hypocrisy, chanting the holy name of Kṛṣṇa is the only means of deliverance.”**

Not by *karma,* not by *jñāna*, not by *yoga*. There is no alternative.

Being infected with the material disease, we require the proper diet as well as the potent medicine to regain our healthy spiritual condition of life. You are amply providing these essential ingredients of the remedy through your instructions to eat only *kṛṣṇa prasādam* and to hear and chant about the unlimited name, fame, form, qualities, and pastimes of the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Your divine books contain the storehouse of Vedic knowledge, which is the only information capable of rectifying all the ills of the world.

Although we cannot claim to be completely freed from our malady, we have experienced a gradual decrease of its symptoms. By following your orders, we have made significant progress towards spiritual health, and we have faith that a total cure is attainable simply by adhering to your Kṛṣṇa conscious method of treatment. And you have assured us that our success is certain as long as we do not deviate from the prescribed path of *sādhana-bhakti.*

You are saving us from drowning in the ocean of material miseries, and thus it is not possible to repay the debt we have incurred by accepting you as our attending physician. The most wonderful feature of your treatment, however, is that the only remuneration you ask from us is that we assist you in the propagation of this sublime cure by distributing the holy name, *prasādam*, and your transcendental books. Therefore it is the duty of all to cooperate in the spreading of Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement for the benefit of all suffering, conditioned souls.

*Your servants at Boston ISKCON,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Nirañjana Swami / Raghunātha Swami / Viṭṭhaleśvara dāsa / Mukta-bandha-dāsa / Dhīra dāsa / Keśīhantā dāsa / Ānanda-maya dāsa / Īśā-devī dāsī

**Grand disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Brisbane

*yoga-yukto viśuddhātmā*  
*vijitātmā jitendriyaḥ*  
*sarva-bhūtātma-bhūtātmā*  
*kurvann api na lipyate*

**”One who works in devotion, who is a pure soul, and who controls his mind and senses is dear to everyone, and everyone is dear to him. Though always working, such a man is never entangled.” (Bg. 5.7)**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In the purport to this verse, you elaborately describe the characteristics of one who is on the path of liberation by Kṛṣṇa consciousness:

He cannot think of any living entity as separate from Kṛṣṇa.  
He is a servant to all because he works in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and thus he is very dear to everyone.  
Everyone is satisfied by his work, as he is pure in consciousness.  
Being pure in consciousness, his mind is completely controlled, and therefore his senses are also controlled.  
Because his mind is fixed in Kṛṣṇa, he does not engage his senses in any matter other than Kṛṣṇa’s service.  
He does not like to hear any topics not relating to Kṛṣṇa, or eat anything not offered to Kṛṣṇa, or go anywhere if Kṛṣṇa is not involved.  
In this way he is never entangled in the reactions of work.

While one may have many lofty ideals and aspire to this topmost platform of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, you always exemplified that pure devotion, and these ideal qualities in fact describe you.

In *Bhagavad-gītā* Lord Kṛṣṇa repeatedly describes the character of one who is transcendental to the three modes of material nature, and who, by engaging in His devotional service, becomes intimately united with Him. This topmost yogī is certainly you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and Lord Kṛṣṇa is singing your glories to all who have the good fortune to hear.

In your earliest years you would go to the Mullik’s temple in Calcutta and offer worship to Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. Your compassion and ability to engage others in the Lord’s service were manifested even in your childhood when you held your first Ratha-yātrā. Of course, later you set even bigger wheels in motion by recreating that Ratha-yātrā throughout the Western world, thus capturing the hearts of all. During your years of schooling, you were an intelligent, inquisitive student, remaining unenamored by

Western ideas, maintaining your pure habits, and, as your friend Rupen recounted, always thinking of “something religious, something philosophical or devotional about God.”

Later, in your marriage, you instructed us on the performance of duty with detachment. In your business life, while you supported your family, you participated extensively and enthusiastically in the activities of the Gauḍiīya Math. Thus, in the performance of all duties and activities, you kept Kṛṣṇa firmly in the center, always planning for the day when you could fulfill your Guru Mahārāja’s wish and your destiny.

From your meeting with Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī, you worked steadily and with the greatest humility to fulfill his desire and Lord Caitanya’s prediction that the holy name would be chanted in every town and village. Then, finally, our good fortune knew no bounds, for you retired from family life and gave your life totally to the upliftment of all living entities by translating the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and tirelessly and fearlessly taking your priceless gifts to the whole world.

You were able to achieve your goal because you were fully surrendered to Kṛṣṇa and never entangled in activities outside of His service. Due to your purity of heart, you understood immediately the desire of your beloved Guru Mahārāja, and with the same purity and immediacy you set about realizing it with certainty and without compromise.

Now as your ISKCON family approaches adolescence, we still acutely feel the absence of your personal presence and are repeatedly hampered by our inability to surrender to Kṛṣṇa; thus it is possible to sometimes become confused, even overwhelmed. However, you are always there for us, as you always have been, offering us the answers. You have given us everything – definitive, illuminating law books, a shining, instructive example, and an abundance of unlimited mercy you so liberally pour on us even when we are undeserving. If we can embrace these gifts and always recognize the reality of your constant loving presence beside us, surely we will become confident, strong and useful ETERNAL servants of you, our loving father.

*Your servants in ISKCON Brisbane, Australia,*

## Brooklyn

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisance at your lotus feet. All glories to Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement, which you are so kindly allowing us to participate in.

Here in New York ISKCON the devotees are happily struggling to continue preaching the glories of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s Holy Name through book distribution, *hari-nāma saṅkīrtana, prasādam* distribution, and regular Sunday and Wednesday programs in the temple. It is always amazing to see how many New Yorkers, both young and old, are pleased to purchase your books, sing in *kīrtana,* take *kṛṣṇa prasādam,* and even dedicate their lives to becoming Kṛṣṇa *bhaktas.*

As you were sometimes dreaming that you had come to New York before your actual arrival, please let us appreciate our advantageous position, being already here. You are *para-duḥkha-duḥkhī;* you had no personal difficulties. But you are *kṛṣṇārpita-prāṇa,* dedicated to the service of Kṛṣṇa, and you are also known as Abhay, “fearless.” So for the purpose of elevating us to devotional service to the Supreme Lord, you entered the Western world, beginning with this most hellish of cities, tolerating every inconvenience, not even knowing if we would ever be able to understand the transcendental message of Vāsudeva.

By your mercy alone we have been able to experience the path of devotional service, and thus we know that there is no other position in life that brings such complete satisfaction to the heart.

We are certainly insignificant, and whatever service we may perform is also very small, but we pray that we may somehow please you by our preaching efforts. And we desire, as you desired on your voyage here, that the transcendental message of Lord Kṛṣṇa may be heard, and that those who hear it become engladdened and thus become liberated from all unhappy conditions of life.

Thank you for giving us the great opportunity of playing a part in distributing that transcendental message. This is our essential service to you.

As stated in *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (5.5.2), *mahat-sevaṁ dvāram āhur vimukteḥ:* “If one desires liberation from the clutches of *māyā,* one must serve a pure devotee, a *mahātmā.* Please let us always remain dedicated to your service and the service of your International Society for Kṛṣṇa Consciousness.

(Written by Lalitā-sakhī-devī dāsī)

Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda Mandira,

## Calcutta

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

All over the world you have traveled.  
The mysteries of devotional service have you unraveled.  
Storehouse of love of Godhead did you breach,  
Saving the whole world from Māyā’s siege.

Extinguishing the blazing fire of material existence  
With the downpour of Kṛṣṇa’s name in full brilliance,  
With karatāla and mṛdaṅga the saṅkīrtana parties you formed,  
Changing the course of the whole world through  
revolutionary reform.

Books are the basis, you have preached:  
Bring the Mahā-bhāgavata within everyone’s reach.  
Prasād distribution you have said will save the soul,  
Although he may be ignorant of life’s real goal.

Chinese, Americans, Greeks, and Russians,  
Assembling in Māyāpura was Bhaktivinoda’s vision.  
You are the pure devotee who fulfilled the prophecy,  
Revealing Śrī Māyāpura-dhāma, Lord Caitanya’s legacy.

A beautiful cultural complex for the world to behold,  
In your birth city soon shall unfold,  
Giving a fresh breath of life to this dying city –  
Causeless mercy of yours and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govindajī.

Calcutta Ratha-yātrā the biggest: your cherished ambition,  
Reviving Calcutta’s glorious age-old tradition.  
The Russians inaugurated Ratha-yātrā, a stupendous achievement,  
Putting the Bengali Marxists into despair and bereavement.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, may your desires become our life and soul.  
In your līlā may we all play an insignificant role.  
Books and prasād to every Calcuttan we meet,  
Dragging them to the shelter of your lotus feet.

*Your most humble servants in Calcutta,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Capetown

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisance at your lotus feet.

Even though constantly embarrassed by our Western bodies and upbringing, we take this opportunity to meditate deeply on your transcendental presence and pastimes. Out of your mercifulness you came to deliver us from the exploitation of scientists, political leaders, teachers, and family members, who are only interested in killing the self.

Having gotten the opportunity to visit your temple in Vṛndāvana, the Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Mandira, and to intently study your books with your disciples and followers, we experienced the real nature of spiritual existence, knowledge, and bliss. We returned to this distant outpost reluctantly, but with the hope of trying to serve you by spreading your mission and your glories.

As we endeavor daily to maintain our consciousness and to distribute your message to others, we realize more and more the insidious nature of *māyā* and the danger of dealing with the material energy without a fully pure desire to engage everything in your service. Gradually and more frequently we are realizing the truth of what you always told us – that chanting the holy name, strictly following the regulative principles, taking *kṛṣṇa-prasādam*, and reading and distributing your books are the only way.

Having missed the opportunity of being directly trained by you, we are bereft of your fierce chastisement and correction when we unwittingly or wittingly go astray.

As the years go by and we try to emulate your exalted qualities, we see ourselves like children trying to wear father’s oversized shoes. We realize that you are truly an ocean of auspicious qualities.

Please help us to see ourselves always in relationship to your instructions and your satisfaction, not our own.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray for your mercy and protection.

Your servants and children in Capetown, South Africa.

## Chandigarh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Most respected Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Kindly accept our humble obeisance unto your divine lotus feet, and bless us that we may continue to have undeviated faith and devotion in the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa through your loving disciples – our spiritual masters.

It is not mere rhetoric for us to say a few words on the occasion of this most auspicious and blessed event, your appearance day, which is observed in ISKCON as the Vyāsa-pūjā day. Rather, it is an expression of our heartfelt gratitude presented in broken words.

With the passing of each year, nay, each day, the significance and import of your appearance and pastimes on this planet become increasingly manifest to us. Our lives would have been doomed, maybe forever, without the intervention of your wonderful institution, ISKCON. It has always been fashionable to say that institutionalization of religion breeds religious corruption; but our experience and the facts are otherwise. When every section of society is meticulously organized, how could religion survive, what to speak of flourish, in isolation, without proper institutional support? Śrīla Prabhupāda! You had the right vision from the beginning. You could foresee that in the late twentieth century, in the age of computers and artificial intelligence, when people are inundated with sensual objects and sense gratification has become the be all and end of all life, devotional service (*bhakti*) cannot be executed by becoming *bābājīs* as in the days of yore. Comprehending the absolute need of association of the devotees (*sādhu-saṅga*) for any tangible progress in spiritual life, Your Divine Grace struggled single-handedly for about seventy years of your life to build a house for all fallen souls of the world – a house that would shield them from the blazing fire of materialism and provide them *sādhu-saṅga* and all other facilities needed to achieve the highest goal of human life, i.e., *kṛṣṇa-prema.* You are the greatest visionary and missionary of recent times, for your dreams have become the reality for the eternal benefit of the entire human race.

Besides, your vision had a down-to-earth practical approach. You could visualize that the materially fertile brains of the rocket-age humans could not be content with the so-called primitive environment associated with religious life. So you not only permitted but also *encouraged* your followers to use their inventions and inventive instincts in the service of the Lord. As a result, we find all sorts of talented persons – writers, engineers, salesmen, agriculturists, artists, painters, musicians, businessmen, technicians, etc. – in your ISKCON. They are utilizing their varied faculties for Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, by writing, printing, and distributing more and more literature on *bhakti-yoga.* They are painting beautiful depictions of the pastimes of the Lord, running *gurukulas* established by Your Divine Grace, planning and building new projects, producing videocassettes and other devotional articles, and running and maintaining the various existing centers, restaurants, farmhouses, etc. All types of skills and aptitudes are being used, through your mercy, only in one profession, i.e., service to the Lord and His holy name. These services provide a unique sense of achievement, fulfillment, and satisfaction not available through any material endeavor.

You are a touchstone, Śrīla Prabhupāda! You have transformed all material occupations into transcendental activities, converted ordinary building and houses into transcendental abodes, and changed the lives of millions from sinfulness to divinity. The glories of the holy name are now being openly sung even in the U.S.S.R – another marvel of your mercy and potency. We take pride in belonging to your transcendental family, and we rededicate ourselves on this auspicious day to your mission of spreading the holy name of the Lord to every town and village under the ever-glittering banner of ISKCON.

*Your loving children and grand children,*

Rādhā-vallabha dāsa / Kṛṣṇa-kāndi dāsa / Nanda Mahārāja dāsa / Sākṣī Gopāla dāsa / and other devotees from ISKCON Chandigarh

## Chicago

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisance in the dust of your lotus feet.

On this most auspicious day we all come together before your *mūrti* form and offer our heartfelt feelings and homage. Certainly your appearance within this material world during this Kali-yuga wasn’t accidental, as some historians believe. “He’s just another cult leader,” these fools say. Rather, you are the Supreme Lord’s pure and empowered representative.

*sākṣād-dharitvena samasta-śāstrair*  
*uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ*  
*kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*

**“The spiritual master is to be honored as much as the Supreme Lord, because he is the most confidential servitor of the Lord. This is acknowledged in all revealed scriptures and followed by all authorities. Therefore I offer my respectful obeisance unto the lotus feet of such a spiritual master, who is a bona fide representative of Śrī Hari \[Kṛṣṇa\].”**

You are just like the moon amongst many stars. It was you only who were empowered to spread the pure teachings of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu to all corners of the world. It was you only who were able to construct a house in which the whole world can live peacefully.

Throughout your stay with us, you set the perfect example of what it means to be fully Kṛṣṇa conscious. You were always trying to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others by constantly preaching. What better way could we show our appreciation and indebtedness to you than by working together within your movement to broadcast the glories of the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa all over the world? Because we are all individuals, certainly there are differences of opinion, and sometimes some friction may occur. However, aren’t we also uncovering our own shortcomings and becoming gradually purified in the meantime?

We simply pray that your divine instructions and example will always appear within our hearts and minds. By meditating in this way, certainly we can unite and actually fulfill your desire of firmly establishing Kṛṣṇa consciousness as the standard way of life for many generations to come.

His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda Kī Jaya!

*Your aspiring servants at the Śrī Śrī Kiśora-Kiśorī Mandira,*

## Christchurch

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances.

All glories to you,  
who came  
fearless and bold,  
To deliver the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu  
To the yavanas and mlecchas.  
Where would we be without you –  
If you had not come on the order of  
Your Guru Mahārāja  
To save and deliver the fallen, conditioned souls?

O ocean of mercy!  
O Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākura!  
Please give us the shade of your lotus feet.  
We have no other shelter  
besides you.  
You are our father, our friend, and  
our ever well-wisher.

In this dark and dismal age of Kali,  
You have given us pure love of God  
freely, by the mercy of Lord Caitanya.

”Chant, dance, and be happy.”  
This is your formula.  
So simple and sublime.  
The perfect cure  
for our chronic disease,  
our Kali-yuga consciousness.

We have taken that medicine.  
We are feeling strong.  
And now we are trying to distribute

The mercy to others –  
Your books, prasādam, the holy name.

Even in your physical absence, so much  
Preaching is going on.  
Thousands of books are being distributed,  
Restaurants serving prasādam, Kṛṣṇa’s mercy, to thousands,  
Nāma-haṭṭa preaching, harināma-saṅkīrtana, lakṣmī liberation –  
No one can escape this great tidal wave of bhakti:  
ISKCON.

Every living entity will hear and taste  
the glories of the Lord.  
Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave us everything.  
What more can we say  
Except “Thank you.”  
Thank you so much  
For your kindness, your mercy, and your love.  
Thank you for giving us Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gauracandra  
to glorify and worship  
twenty-four hours a day.  
Thank you for giving us so many advanced  
Vaiṣṇavas to associate with and help us on our  
Journey back home, back to Godhead.  
But most of all, thank you for giving us the  
Opportunity to take part in this wonderful ISKCON.

So please continue to bless us with the  
Desire, the strength, and the enthusiasm  
To assist you in carrying out your mission,  
Which is the same as Lord Caitanya’s:  
”Wherever you go, whomever you meet,  
Tell them about Kṛṣṇa,”  
All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, Patita-pāvana!  
Savior of the fallen souls.

*We remain your humble servants at Christchurch, New Zealand,*
