## Israel

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisance at your lotus feet. All glories to you.

By your mercy, the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu is spreading to the anxiety-filled residents of Israel. Other than taking shelter of your lotus feet and chanting the holy name of Kṛṣṇa, it is very clear that there is no solution to the ever-increasingly entangling web of problems that plague the population, amongst whom the bodily conception of life is grossly prominent. This year we’ve witnessed a remarkable breakthrough in the field of preaching to Arabic peoples. Arab Druze, Christian, and even Muslims are eagerly purchasing sets of Your Divine Grace’s transcendental books. Nāma-haṭṭa groups are forming in their villages. The intellectuals of their communities are taking a serious interest in Vaiṣṇava philosophy and culture.

Simultaneously, in the center of a Westernized city like Tel Aviv, we are experiencing how all peoples are attracted to your ISKCON movement. Jews and Arabs alike are aroused from their material slumber by your mercy in the form of the *mahā-mantra,* distribution of your books, and distribution of *kṛṣṇa-prasādam.* We pray for your mercy to awaken these unfortunate people to their original Kṛṣṇa consciousness, thus freeing them from their heavy attachment to bodily designations and bringing them peace to the Middle East. There is no other way. There is no other way. There is no other way.

Of course, we know that real peace will come when we are very serious about our own spiritual lives and make our own hearts very pure. This means having full faith in your words and in the process you have given us. We beg you to guide us to be able to cooperate amongst ourselves, free from false ego, and to give up our own bodily designations. Only in this way can we please you. Please help us to represent you properly and to glorify you by our actions, words, and thoughts.

We once heard an attempted blasphemy of Your Divine Grace by some of those unscrupulous people who foolishly take it upon themselves to stop your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. They said that you did not actually write all of the books, for it is not possible for one man to accomplish such a task. Just as the commentators on the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* sometimes perceive how such demons as Hiraṇyākṣa unintentionally glorify the Lord with their insults, so we could understand that these demons were actually glorifying you. Your activities and accomplishments are inconceivable to the materialists; neither are they possible for one in the material world.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, keep us always as your servants, and please let us engage life after life in the *saṅkīrtana* movement. We pray to have no other desire.

*Your servants from the Israel yātrā,*

Disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Guṇa-avatāra dāsa

**Grand disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Japan

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisance at your lotus feet.

Lord Caitanya explains, “The *jīva* souls are covered with ignorance because of forgetting their relationship with Kṛṣṇa.” Since we are covered with ignorance, we are not at the stage where we can serve in the mood of separation. However, if we can understand even a drop of how you are serving in the mood of separation from Kṛṣṇa – through meditating on your activities and your lotus-flower – like nectarine words, which are sometimes as hard as Lord Nṛsiṁha’s nails – our lives will be perfect.

Whatever trouble comes, whether *adhyātmika, adhibhautika,* or *adhidaivika,* it’s impossible to stop distributing books. Books are Kṛṣṇa Himself personally, so they’re very powerful. Books themselves go out and are flying.

In the Japanese *yātrā,* our Kurukṣetra, we’ve been fighting with Māyā, who covers people’s hearts. Only book distribution can smash all the darkness of ignorance in Kali-yuga.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us just a millionth part of the taste for the holy name possessed by yourself and such other great devotees as Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī, who prays,

*tuṇḍe tāṇḍavinī ratiṁ vitanute tuṇḍāvalī-labdhaye*  
*karṇa-kroḍa-kaḍambinī ghaṭayate karṇārbudebhyaḥ spṛhām*  
*cetaḥ-prāṅgaṇa-saṅginī vijayate sarvendrdiyāṇāṁ kṛtiṁ*  
*no jāne janitā kiyadbhir amṛtaiḥ kṛṣṇeti varṇa-dvayī*

**”I do not know how much nectar the two syllables** ***‘kṛṣ-ṇa’*** **have produced. When the holy name of Kṛṣṇa is chanted, it appears to dance within the mouth. We then desire many, many mouths. When that name enters the holes of the ears, we desire many millions of ears. And when the holy name dances in the courtyard of the heart, it conquers the activities of the mind, and therefore all the senses become inert.” (Cc.** ***Antya-līlā*** **1.120)**

The *saṅkīrtana* movement is distributing this nectarine ocean, the fruits of love of God, and limitless ecstasy to people infinitely. And behind, there are regulative principles we must follow strictly, and the spiritual master’s order we must also follow. We are so stupid that we cannot follow even the basic *sādhana.* We can measure our love for you by seeing how much we can follow your instructions sincerely and seriously.

You spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world within a few years and still left us service to maintain and increase your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Purity is the force. Now we understand that the activity of thousands of devotees cannot be compared with your own power. We just pray that we can take shelter of your books, which are a manifestation of your mercy, and that we can keep distributing your protection to people! No matter how many books we distribute as an offering to you, they cannot be compared to a drop of the ocean. Only by your grace, which is like the ocean, can we keep distributing your books unlimitedly.

In this Kali-yuga, the places which do not have your books are simply cemeteries.

*Your servants at the Japan Yātrā,*

## Johannesburg

*nama oṁ viṣṇu pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate devam gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisance at the dust of your lotus feet, which are the only shelter in the age of Kali.

The other day, a group of devotees were going through the Twelfth Canto of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* and we came across a painting that really caught our attention. The painting was of a boat sailing through the ocean, rescuing the drowning living entities attacked by waves of illusion.

At the helm of the boat was Your Divine Grace, arms upraised, inviting the conditioned souls to come aboard the boat of ISKCON and journey back home, back to Godhead. This is the mission of Your Divine Grace – to fulfill the desires of your Guru Mahārāja and of the previous *ācāryas.*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we see that through your constant preaching efforts, people all over the world are associating with the devotees and with Your Divine Grace through your transcendental gifts of the world – your books, which are the law books for mankind.

By your perfect example you showed us how to preach and how to practice Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and thus you are the spiritual master of the entire world.

You are endowed with all the qualities of a Vaiṣṇava, and with great humility you are presenting the message of the *Bhāgavata,* following in the footsteps of the previous *ācāryas.* Your translating of this timeless knowledge for the benefit of the suffering masses is the sign of your compassion. You are giving us your association through *vāṇī* and *vapu.*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this most auspicious occasion we beg for your mercy so that we may continue with great patience and enthusiasm to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness and to distribute your books, together with the holy name and *prasādam.*

Śrīla Prabhupāda-kī Jaya!

*Your fallen servants in Johannesburg, South Africa,*

## Laguna Beach

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humbly offered obeisances at your feet eternally.

*bhavantam evānucaran nirantaraḥ*  
*praśānta-niḥśesa-mano-rathāntaraḥ*  
*kadāham aikāntika-nitya-kiṅkaraḥ*  
*praharṣayiśyāmi sa-nātha-jīvitam*

**"By serving you constantly, one is freed from all material desires and thus is completely pacified. When shall we engage as your permanent eternal servitors and always feel joyful to have such a perfect master?"**

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are trying to come up to your standards in every aspect of our lives. We know that this is the formula for real success.

We know that by doubting and not acting purely, we remain in the material world, suffering from material desires and miseries. We pray simply to serve you constantly and permanently so that we may not only achieve happiness ourselves but give it to others, which is the real art of devotional service. In giving Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others, we pray that we will not inject our own flaws into the process. All success depends on purity and cleanliness.

We rededicate ourselves to being clean and purely in line with your instructions so that we may have the opportunity to be called your servants. Foolishly, we often forget how great is your mission and become overwhelmed by all types of mental problems. But when we remember just a fraction of your glory, we become excited and joyful to engage as your servants, knowing you are with us.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we cannot ask you to do our surrendering for us. We can only hope that, through the power of your mercy and our own conviction, we shall see your greatness and thus serve you with full intelligence and freedom from the cheating propensity. The future is up to us, and you have laid the path. Our task is simply not to deviate.

All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda! Let us work to our highest potential to surrender to your excellent guidance.

*Your aspiring servants at New Jhārikhaṇḍa Mandira (Laguna Beach).*

## Lansing

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances on this auspicious day of your appearance, as we relish hearing your devotees glorify your unlimited transcendental qualities.

In your genuine concern that your disciples and followers not be led astray, you often expose the unscrupulous rascals within your Bhaktivedanta purports. Somehow, this exposing is done in a most poetic way, free of all sentimentality, and expresses your unprecedented compassion:

In the modem age there is a tendency to do research by mental speculation and concoction. But the man who speculates forgets that he himself is subject to the four defects of nature. . . . Unless one has perfect knowledge from the disciplic succession, he simply puts forth some theories of his own creation; therefore he is cheating people. *(Bhāg.* 3.24.17)

We do not know how people can be captivated by the bogus *yoga* system in which it is stated that simply by meditating fifteen minutes daily one can attain the perfection of becoming one with God. This age of Kali-yuga is the age of bluffing and quarrel. Actually there is no possibility of attaining *yoga* perfection by such paltry proposals. *(Bhāg.* 3.21.6)

Meditation upon voidness is a modem invention of the fertile brain of some speculator. *(Bhāg.* 3.26.28)

Our materialistic tendency is to be cheaters, fools, and rascals while simultaneously being cheated by the fools and rascals. But you have delivered the panacea for all distress and problems—Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We thank you again and again, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

*Your eternal servants in Lansing, Michigan,*

Apūrva dāsa / Kamalinī-devī dāsī / Pañca-tattva and Amala-purāṇa

## Lima

*ācāryaṁ māṁ vijānīyān*  
*nāvamanyeta karhicit*  
*na martya-bhuddhyāsūyeta*  
*sarva-deva-mayo guruḥ*

**"One should know the** ***ācārya*** **as Myself and never disrespect him in any way. One should not envy him, thinking him an ordinary man, fir he is the representative of all the demigods."**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Let us offer our humble obeisances at our lotus feet.

We receive again the wonderful opportunity to glorify you and to thank you for having given us the most valuable gift, Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

But of course, to be able to glorify you correctly, we need your inspiration. Only a great soul, a pure devotee of the Lord like yourself, has the capacity to glorify and properly honor another great personality, as you did in such an extraordinary way during the appearance ceremony of your spiritual master in February 1936. Your potent speech on that occasion must have amazed and enlightened everybody present and left them without breath. Only someone who perfectly understands the real meaning of the *Vedas,* of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, of the *guru-disciple* relationship, and of the urgent need to save the conditioned souls of the Kali-yuga, according to the desire of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, could pronounce those eloquent and illuminating words, so full of truth.

For all of the work you have done for our benefit and the benefit of all humanity, we know that you are also that Ācāryadeva and Jagat Guru, to whom we offer our humble homage.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for teaching us to appreciate the transcendental qualities of the pure devotees of the Lord. Thank you for teaching us to appreciate Kṛṣṇa consciousness and for giving us enthusiasm to reach the goal. Let us overcome every obstacle in your service to you so that we can repay you in some way for all that you have given us.

Thank you again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and please accept us as your humble servants.

*Your fallen servants in Lima, Peru.*

## London

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. We learn from the prayers of Śrīla Viśvanātha Cakravartī Ṭhākura that praising the spiritual master is something the disciple should always do. Indeed, by remembering and following the instructions of our spiritual master, we obtain the mercy of the Supreme Lord. We are singing every day that the lotus feet of the spiritual master are the only way to obtain pure devotional service.

You have also written that the disciple must pass the test of the spiritual master in order to obtain the lotus feet of the Lord. What is that test? What are you asking of us?

You are asking us to give up all things detrimental to achieving fulfillment of this rare human form. That a human being is willing to struggle and suffer some inconvenience in the process of self-restraint surely distinguishes him from the beasts, who simply eat, sleep, mate and defend without restraint. You are asking us to follow the regulative principles, and just as you mentioned that Kṛṣṇa never asked Arjuna to do anything impractical, surely you are asking us to do what every human being can do if he is serious to get out of this miserable material world.

For us in the West it is easy to appreciate your gifts because you are the only pure devotee to cross the ocean to this previously godforsaken place—the industrialized Western world, where hedonism is backed by philosophy, and where religion retreats into the corner of compromise and impotence. With the sword of the *paramparā* you have cut through the intertwined woods of ignorance and created a path for those who want it—a path to the light of day.

You have handed that sword to your disciples, who at first were a little unsure how to hold such a powerful weapon. Let us constantly remember what you did, how you behaved, what were you priorities, how merciful you were, and how you refused to compromise in the face of so-called necessity. Let carrying out your desires be our goal, and if we feel unable, at least let that be the standard toward which we struggle.

Let us never forget that if we are Kṛṣṇa conscious our whole existence will be blessed and we shall want for nothing; we shall be free of the pains of old age and death. But more than that, let us never forget who suffered and struggled to give the wretches of the Western world this opportunity. Without you we would not have seen the truth; without you we would not have been able to obtain the greatest treasure of the lotus feet of the Lord. Without you we would still be trying to adjust the material nature in a vain struggle for happiness. Surely no one can doubt that you are our savior, our holy hope, our object of eternal worship.

When you laughed, our hearts would fly, free from all moroseness, and we would dance for joy. When you spoke the Kṛṣṇa conscious philosophy of *Bhagavad-gītā* our intelligence would soar. All other so-called philosophers had simply covered such self-effulgent truth by their speculations. Even when you left us, you left behind your separated energies—your taped lectures, your Bhaktivedanta purports, and your ISKCON—to give shelter to and to deliver the poor souls.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are an ocean of love, bliss, knowledge, and kindness. Only our foolish attempts to find water in this material desert stop us from living with you in that ocean. Only the steel frames around our hearts stop us from swimming in that ocean and adding to its limitless depths our tears of love. You have given us the holy name and the process of devotional service. As water wears down a stone, one day, by following your instructions, we will all meet again and we will be pure—able to share in love of your Kṛṣṇa. When, oh, when, will that day be ours?

*Your insignificant servants,*  
*the devotees of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Londonīśvara,*

## Los Angeles

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious day of your appearance,  
Only you could force Kali to cry "Interference!"  
Were it not for your coming to this godforsaken land,  
Surely there would be no hope to be free of Māyā’s hand.

Coming with just the bare necessities and your precious books,  
You shattered all our lives' complexities by your saintly looks.  
Preaching with undying faith Lord Caitanya's causeless message,  
You've provided for everyone, even the unknowing, safe passage.

*For Kṛṣṇa you provided everything, for yourself a straw in the street.*  
In San Francisco I tried but was too embarrassed to touch your lotus feet.  
You purified everyone by your words and association.  
*Our purpose to serve Kṛṣṇa and Guru!, not some security and lose the mission.*

We've lost some enthusiasm over the years.  
Māyā's influence is such that we think we have no fears.  
Time is passing and the body's growing older,  
But your example was to become bolder and bolder.

Defeating the scientists and philosophers of the West,  
You are by far, Prabhupāda, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta’s best.  
Preaching, singing, cooking, dancing—everything you did was the best example.  
Just to get near you people would push, and one could get trampled.

You gave your mercy and always tolerated our offenses.  
To come in contact with someone like you—what are the chances?  
We were struggling in this world under the pretense of some harmony.  
Your point was that we're more than just this body.

How long will it take for us to realize you're our real father,  
And that in this world there's no happiness without each other?  
You always stressed that strength was like a bundle of sticks,  
So there's no real safety if we think, "I'll just mix."

Now is the time for us to band and work together  
"For the benefit of this suffering and misdirected humanity," as you said in one letter.  
Our main business is preaching and distributing your books.  
Others may be teaching but are revealed to be crooks.

We have a choice: to jump on the train and feel the ecstasy,  
Or just be association for each other's misery.  
There's no sense in struggling and trying to squeeze some juice,  
Because this world's like a barren desert—there are no fruits!

Please help us become serious and devout, and for us in your humility always pray,  
For without your mercy what hope is there for us? We'll just return to our dismay.  
We've tried everything, so what will it take for us to learn  
That without your service and Kṛṣṇa's protection life is just a slow burn.

Sometimes we may seem sincere, but later we realize this isn't real.  
Always motivated, like a thief in disguise, we never show what we really feel.  
Pretending to be your servants, we make a show of devotion.  
Only when we give up material desires will there be real emotion.

Your books are the real offering; if we read them they will act.  
Their primary instruction: "Kṛṣṇa's our real shelter"—that's the fact.  
"My first duty is to serve him; how could I leave his service?" Prahlāda said.  
Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us your mercy and take us back to Godhead.

*Your servants at the Los Angeles temple.*

## Malayasia

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances at your lotus feet.

How fortunate we are here that you graced this country in 1971 by your presence. Unfortunately, you did not come again, and your *vāṇī* was hardly served. Years passed by before a serious attempt was made to install your *vāṇī* here.

Then in 1979 the seeds of your visit began to grow. Unfortunately, those beautiful flowering buds flourishing on your *vāṇī* were smashed by the mad elephants. As a result, so many weeds quickly took advantage of the devastation, making it very hard for the creeper of pure *bhakti* to blossom. In your personal presence such weeds would be quickly shown up and dealt with, to protect the precious *bhakti-latā.* Now the scene is not so clear. Your absence, like the absence of the sun, gives an opportunity for darkness to enter.

How fortunate we are that you are still here in your *vāṇī,* especially in your books. How unfortunate we are, still, not to take shelter of you in this form. As the sages were perplexed as to how religious principles would be protected after Lord Kṛṣṇa' s disappearance, we have also been perplexed in your absence. Our independent, speculative, envious tendencies battle with each other for supremacy. The sages' doubts were cleared up by Sūta Gosvāmī. Our doubts can be cleared up when we take shelter of your books and worship your *vāṇī.* You told us that everything is there in your books. Instead of looking elsewhere for solutions, we should understand without a doubt that our only hope for working together to cultivate the Vaikuṇṭha garden and root out the weeds is to take full shelter of your books in every respect.

How unfortunate that we do not take full advantage of your *vāṇī.* How unfortunate that far too often we ignore or even deprecate your *vāṇī,* thinking we know better. How unfortunate that the conditioned souls, instead of receiving the pure *bhakti-latā,* get entangled in hodge-podge, speculative, watered-down *anarthas.* We therefore beg for shelter at your lotus feet, so that we ourselves may become fortunate by firmly holding on to your *vāṇī* and not allowing any weeds to suck the nourishment from our *bhakti-latā* or parasites to strangle it. Please give us the strength to uproot the weeds and eradicate the parasites. Please give us the good fortune to see everywhere the darkness being dissipated again and the enthusiastic light of your presence removing all ignorance. We pray that we may become your instruments to protect the sacred message and not be parasites.

Begging your forgiveness for our failing to live by your instructions, we pray to take shelter of your *vāṇī* and to hold your books as our eternal guides.

*Your servants at ISKCON Malaysia.*

## Manchester

Gurudeva!  
By a drop of thy pure mercy,  
bestow on us humility,  
that we sincerely feel far less  
than even lowly growing grass.  
Give us strength to be thy servant  
when faced with trials and with torment,  
and teach us never to expect  
all recognition and respect.

O lord and master, we here pray  
for thy power, that through each day  
we may never fail to offer  
to all creatures proper honor.  
Only then in loving feeling  
for the Lord's pure name revealing,  
shall we chant unremittingly,  
from all offense forever free.

O lord and master, we beseech  
thy blessings now upon us each;  
we are low and lacking wisdom,  
weak and without resolution;  
see our poor and piteous plight,  
dear lord, pray lift us that we might,  
attending on thy wish and whim,  
beloved servants thus become.

Examining ourselves, we find  
qualities of the lowest kind,  
thus thy mercy is essential,  
and it is our one credential;  
if thy mercy is not given,  
bereft of hope to be delivered,  
till death we will but weep and weep,  
no reason left for life to keep.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our repeated obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. By your infinite kindness we have passed another year in the struggle to be Kṛṣṇa conscious and to give Kṛṣṇa consciousness to others. Alas, though, we come again with nothing to offer you but accounts of continuous failure. Due to our clinging stubbornly to the bodily conception of life, we have done little more than create a disturbance in your society. Please forgive us. Our efforts to be your peons are nothing short of pathetic, as we fall repeated victims to our voracious sense desires. Indeed, we lament greatly at this sad truth and even more greatly at the fact that no remorse or regret arises within us. We lack even the honest desire to sincerely pray for your mercy, so that you may cut away our ignorance. We fully accept nescience and misery as pleasure. This is all known to you, and yet you allow us to remain in your society.

Your instructions are our only hope. Somehow—we cannot fathom how or why—we have contacted those instructions and are trying to accept them as the light of our wretched lives. We never saw you, and you did not initiate us, but somehow we have become charged with your desire, and this amazes us. How could anyone be so kind? By your incredible power we know of no other alternative but to strive for the success of your mission. Yet we are blind and incapable. Our hearts are filled with blackness and deceit, but your strong will has captured us. We feel we would rather die than be taken from your service, although we are totally unable to prove ourselves useful in any way whatsoever. Such is the mystery of your causeless mercy.

Having seen your kindness thus far, we feel that it will prevail and that you will not send us away. If so, then we are confident that the power of the Lord's holy name will break through the steel walls of our hearts and cleanse away our ignorance. Please free us from offenses, especially to your other sincere servants and to your glorious service itself. We can offer in return only the promise that we will continue to try to become your real servants, although at present that seems to be lifetimes away. Thanking you again and again, we beg to remain

*Your aspiring servants in Manchester, U.K.,*

Kṛṣṇa-dharma dāsa / Abhaya dāsa / Guru-dakṣiṇa dāsa / Keśava dāsa / Cintāmaṇi-dhāma-devī dāsī / Bhakta Damien / Bhakta Sukdev / Bhakta Baldev / Bhakta Yoga / Bhakta Rick / Bhaktin Laxmi / Bhaktin Irene

## Mauritius

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, and all glories to your movement and all its followers.

We are very happy to render some devotional service here in Mauritius to spread your movement. All the success we have had until now is due to your mercy, and all the success we shall have in the future will also come because we are following your instructions. We have no other way to get the blessings of the *paramparā* system and Kṛṣṇa. As Lord Kṛṣṇa is one, so His movement is also one. Please give us intelligence, patience, and all good qualities so that we can convince everybody about this fact. Of course, as it is said, "Charity begins at home." So we are all praying to be ourselves fully cooperative with this movement. Kṛṣṇa instructs us that "as we surrender unto Him, He will reward us accordingly." So let us be more and more dependent on this movement by following your instructions more and more strictly. Then only we can succeed. And thus the entire society can benefit and blossom, and everybody will be chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa, taking *prasādam,* and serving the Lord without anxiety, in full ecstasy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-ki Jaya! The *saṅkīrtana* movement-ki Jaya! All glories to the Vaiṣṇavas! Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Hoping to remain the servants of your servants,

*Your devotees in Mauritius.*

## Māyāpura

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

O master of my dark and churning heart, as this, another day, begins, I beg to pay you the most sincere obeisances that such a soul as I may offer. I will never forget the feelings that you have made me feel—that I can honestly say. And I will always remember that evening in Śrīdhām Māyāpura when, as a guard outside your quarters, I stood in the darkness watching you as you sat peacefully chanting. I was sure that you could not see me, for your room was filled with bright light and I stood in total darkness. As I stood there, my mind began to compose glorifications of you, and I felt as if my heart would burst with the love and appreciation I felt for you. Tears filled my eyes. I saw you as the greatest, purest, most exalted personality I had ever met in all my billions of repeated births. I saw you as the greatest personality who had ever graced this planet—indeed, THE UNIVERSE! And I saw you sitting there, the most humble, gentle servant of Kṛṣṇa Himself, and I was awed beyond words to realize that I was standing in the presence of one so very dear to, so intimately related to. Lord Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Tears came from my eyes, and in my heart I yearned to go before you and fall at your feet and tell you I loved you and that I'd give my whole life for you—as well as all the future ones—but I dared not disturb you. Within my heart a great struggle was taking place, for it was bursting with my desire to tell you all that I had realized, and these seemed to me to be the most precious, glorious moments that I must ever have experienced during my entire sojourn in the material creation. But how could I disturb you, who were sitting perfectly tranquil on your *asana,* meditating most assuredly on how best to bless all the souls of the world with Kṛṣṇa consciousness? Sol stood there in the darkness, yearning, burning to say I loved you but daring not even to show myself, and suddenly you looked up, straight into my eyes, and you smiled a radiant smile that told me without a doubt that you knew all that I was thinking and you appreciated my offering. And everything that I had felt up to that moment became insignificant beside the realization that you knew my heart better than I myself, and I prostrated myself on the floor outside your window in gratitude and jubilation.

You have worked a great and glorious miracle within my cavernous heart, and even though that miracle remains as yet incomplete, it is a shining ray of hope to me. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are like the sun, illuminating with hope the dark hearts of the countless numbers of souls you will always continue to save.

*Your unworthy servants at Śrī Māyāpura-candrodaya Mandira.*

## Melbourne

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Sometimes new devotees lament that they were not able to have your personal association. "I am so unfortunate to have been bom too late," they say, for from the external point of view, beloved Guru Maharaja, you have left this planet.

We know that since the spiritual master is present in his teachings, you are present in your books. But you seem to be personally present in further ways:

When the *harindma* party dances through the streets of Melbourne, roaring "Hare Kṛṣṇa! Hare Kṛṣṇa!" the conditioned souls are surprised, amazed, and attracted;

When two hundred Sunday feast guests line up for "seconds," eager for another serving of curd *subjī, pakorās,* sweetrice, or *halavā prasādam;*

When we receive a bus excursion of eager and inquisitive students from a Melbourne school and guide them through the temple and grounds, reveal some of the tenets of the *Bhagavad-gītā,* give them books, and serve them *prasādam;*

When another prize-winning Hare Kṛṣṇa Moomba float glides down the center of Melbourne, past the smiling faces of the state premier, the mayor, and city council members, within the view of hundreds of thousands of others, both on the street and in their homes, watching the parade on television;

When the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa echoes between the city's buildings as the devotees leading the float dance, their hands held high, and the TV commentators tell their audiences that the devotees chant Hare Kṛṣṇa to glorify God;

When the people of Melbourne throng around the float after the parade, clamor for bags of popcorn *prasādam,* reach out for books, or pose for the family album with a devotee on the float;

When we observe the patrons of Gopal's restaurant, across the street from the town hall, reading *Back to Godhead* magazine as they dote over their favorite *prasādam;*

When we taste the *prasādam* and smile, knowing that it is the very best;

When we see Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Vallabha, and Śrī Śrī Jagannātha, Baladeva, and Subhadrā in their multicolored, excellent clothing, with garlands of fresh, fragrant flowers around their necks as they shower benedictions upon the devotees with mercy, accepting their humble offerings and prayers;

And when we see new devotees, with freshly shaven heads or colorful *sārīs,* chanting the holy names of Kṛṣṇa, bright and beaming with enthusiasm, tasting a newly found nectar.

These devotees are not unfortunate. They are the most fortunate, for they are here in your personal association, where everything is happening by your grace. It is you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, who are playing the *bṛhat-mṛdaṅga,* blessing the conditioned souls with Kṛṣṇa's mercy And the gate is wide open on the path back to Godhead for all devotees who please you by serving your mission. What greater fortune can anyone have than this?

*Your hopeful servants in Melbourne,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Mexico City

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances.

While reading your *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* we came across a very nice *śloka* from the third canto, in the conversation between Vidura and Maitreya. We like this particular *śloka* because it describes how we feel:

*arthābhāvaṁ viniścitya*  
*pratītasyāpi nātmanaḥ*  
*tāṁ cāpi yuṣmac-caraṇa-*  
*sevayāhaṁ parāṇude*

**"My dear sir, I am obliged to you because now I can understand that this material manifestation is without substance, although it appears real. I am confident that by serving your feet it will be possible to give up the false idea." (*****Bhāg.*** **3.7.18)**

You state in your purport of the above *śloka:* "The sufferings of the conditioned soul are superficial and have no intrinsic value, like the cutting off of one's head in a dream. Yet although this statement is theoretically very true, it is very difficult for the common man or the neophyte on the transcendental path to realize practically."

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are the "neophytes on the transcendental path" who you describe in your purport, so we have no practical realization. But we are confident that if we serve you, that realization will come. Therefore on this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā *tithi,* we are offering our humble worship at your lotus feet by putting together a very nice *saṅkīrtana* party, which goes out every day and passes out many, many books.

Also, we are trying to behave properly, as you have instructed, to show the people in general that the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, has blessed us with your wonderful, transcendental association. Our lives will be successful if we can exhibit just a minute fraction of your transcendental qualities, which are those of the truly saintly persons described in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* by Kapila Muni to His mother Devahūti, as follows:

*titikṣavaḥ kāruṇikāḥ*  
*suhṛdaḥ sarva-dehinām*  
*ajāta-śatravaḥ śāntāḥ*  
*sādhavaḥ sādhu-bhūṣaṇāḥ*

**"The symptoms of a** ***sādhu*** **are that he is tolerant, merciful, and friendly to all living entities. He has no enemies, he is peaceful, he abides by the scriptures, and all his characteristics are sublime."** ***(Bhāg.*** **3.25.21)**

It is described in the *śāstra* that by the proper association one may develop good qualities and get freedom from the material existence:

*prasaṅgam ajaraṁ pāśam*  
*ātmanaḥ kavayo viduḥ*  
*sa eva sādhuṣu kṛto*  
*mokṣa-dvāram apāvṛtam*

**"Every learned man knows very well that attachment for the material is the greatest entanglement of the spirit soul. But that same attachment, when applied to the self-realized devotees, opens the door of liberation."** ***(Bhāg.*** **3.25.20)**

Therefore, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, although we are so attached to the material world, by good fortune we have gotten your transcendental association, and by that association we can develop the transcendental qualities and the purity we need to help you spread this *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu throughout this country.

It is stated in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam:*

*bhaktyā pumāñ jāta-virāga aindriyād*  
*dṛṣṭa-śrutān mad-racanānucintayā*  
*cittasya yatto grahaṇe yoga-yukto*  
*yatiṣyate ṛjubhir yoga-mārgaiḥ*

**"Thus consciously engaged in devotional service in the association of the devotees, a person gains distaste for sense gratification, both in this world and in the next, by constantly thinking about the activities of the Lord. This process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness is the easiest process of mystic power; when one is actually situated on that path of devotional service, he is able to control the mind."** ***(Bhāg.*** **3.25.26)**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we have accepted you as our guide on the path back to Godhead. Please give us the strength to be able to follow in your footsteps and engage steadily in your transcendental service by distributing your transcendental literature. We know that this will give you transcendental pleasure, and that this service will assure us of always being situated in the safe position at your lotus feet in this material world. You come from the abode of Kṛṣṇa, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and therefore you can take us back there. Indeed, you are showing us the path to that transcendental abode by the means of the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Beloved Śrīla Prabhupāda, on this auspicious day we, your disciples and granddisciples, make this humble offering at your divine lotus feet. Please accept it. We bow down to you, our only shelter.

Vyāsa-pūjā *tithi-kī jaya*!

(written by Rāma-Govinda B. G. Goswami)

*Your aspiring servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-gopāla Mandira, Mexico City,*

## Miami

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you simply tried to fulfill the order of your spiritual master in a humble way, as Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu has advised us: *gopī-bhartuḥ pada-kamalayor dāsa-dāsānudāsaḥ:* "One must be the servant of the servant of the servant of Lord Kṛṣṇa."

This is the real proof of your pure devotion to Kṛṣṇa and your unlimited compassion. We are your servants, and we beg your causeless mercy on this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā. We pray at your lotus feet that you will bestow your mercy upon us.

You implanted in our hearts faith in the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Kṛṣṇa. It is this faith which is giving us strength to serve you day after day.

Because of your efforts, we, whose minds were engaged in sense gratification, are now keeping Vaiṣṇava principles and preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world. As you are all-pervading, we need your continuous assistance in this regard so that we can carry out your mission, which is nondifferent from Lord Caitanya's mission:

*pṛthivīte āche yata nagarādi grāma*  
*sarvatra pracāra haibe mora nāma*  
*\[CB Antya-khaṇḍa 4.126\]*

On this most auspicious day of your appearance, we pray for your blessings to enable us to remain totally engaged in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and constantly preaching your glories.

*Your servants at the Miami Mandir,*

## Miami Beach

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Very dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances. You have told us, "I shall remain your personal guide whether I am physically present or not present, just as I am getting personal guidance from my Guru Maharaja." Indeed, you have not left us; you are still here by sound, by your instructions; you have only left our mundane vision.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our eternal guide. Once you were asked if you would be our spiritual master eternally, and you replied, *"Cakhu-dān dilo ye, janme janme pitā set:* The one who has opened my eyes—he is my father life after life."

When you were preparing to depart from this world in Vṛndāvana, you were asked if we will be with you when we die. You answered, "When you go to Vaikuṇṭha, the residents of Kṛṣṇaloka will comment, 'Oh, look at the nice *guru* and his disciples.' "

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us to feel your presence at every moment, dictating to us the proper course of action so that we may become better instruments in executing your will.

You said that preaching means to voluntarily accept botheration. "Unless I took botheration, how this movement could have been established? I was living very peacefully in Vṛndāvana. When you want to do something big, you must take botheration. It cannot be done peacefully. You have to meet with people of so many mentalities and adjust everything. Kṛṣṇa has taken so much botheration: He has to sit in everyone's heart and try how to make this rascal Kṛṣṇa conscious. Sometimes He uses force, sometimes kindness." So you are teaching us "that sacrifice doesn't just mean maintenance. The sacrifice that really counts is when our inconvenience is not just for our personal maintenance, but when our inconvenience comes for spreading this movement. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the strength and conviction to push on this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement all over the world.

You taught us that material education isn't the solution to the world's problems: the criminals simply become more sophisticated. Service to humanity means giving people knowledge, *jñāna.* That is the highest service. You gave everyone access to the Absolute Truth, and that is the real understanding of the desire to serve humanity. You broadcast the actual standard of religion: unalloyed devotional service. In Kali-yuga there is so much deviation from what is right and true, but you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are like the sun in that you purified all this aberration with your impeccable character. As purity is the force, so impurity will make us weak against the onslaughts of *māyā.* Please help us maintain your standard of purity, which is the unique asset of your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

You tell us, "Our books are keeping alive this movement." We are distributing the books on their merit, and we are finding that people want these books. Here in Miami, our airport has been open almost fifteen years for straight book distribution. Please help us to depend upon Lord Kṛṣṇa more and more so that we will have the fortitude not just for full-time but for full *life*-time service at your lotus feet.

*Your servants in Miami Beach,*

## Nairobi

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our humble obeisances unto your lotus feet.  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
On this day your children and grandchildren pray  
That your mercy remain with us  
By our following your footsteps.  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Let us all cooperate to spread your mission  
And the holy name,  
And Maya's kingdom of illusion will go away.  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Books are your weapons for fighting  
The darkness of ignorance of the Kali-yuga men.  
We are your instruments to fight again and again.  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Your glorious Bhaktivedanta purports  
Conquer the world to and fro,  
Teaching the so-called scientists, religious leaders,  
And politicians what they don't know.  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Taking Kṛṣṇa and guru with you,  
The holy land of Vṛndāvana you have left,  
Showing the bābājīs, svāmīs and gurus  
How Kṛṣṇa consciousness has to be presented.  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
You challenged the atheists; they could not stand;  
They could not defeat your arguments.  
They were simply sad.  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Just like a lion of the guru-paramparā,  
You smash the cheaters and rascals  
Who claim to be God.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Hypocrisy and cheating is the game of this age.  
Your challenge to the world and your books remain.  
Still you are present, you are not dead;  
You left this world to live and spread the holy name.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
You have given us a new life and hope.  
Our life is in ignorance without knowledge of God,  
But to you we are praying to rescue us from this material world.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
In Kali-yuga, the godless age,  
You have created an army for fighting with Māyā  
From your ISKCON base.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
The attacks of Māyā come in many ways,  
Taking us far from you.  
Please don't let us go, but let us stay.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
ISKCON is your body, the BBT your heart,  
And anyone who is empowered should serve you  
By establishing in every town and village harināma-saṅkīrtana.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
This is the challenge that you have given to us:  
How to present Kṛṣṇa consciousness  
By following your art.  
Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!  
Your temples, devotees, and books are there to help.  
These are our only shelter in this miserable age.  
Let us give up pride and greed and be ready to serve.  
Let us not fight among ourselves but cooperate.  
Then our real love will be shown for Your Divine Grace.  
Today we pray to remain as your servants—  
Please don't let us fade away.  
*Your fallen servants from ISKCON Nairobi, Kenya,*

## Nava Jiyada-nṛsiṁha-kṣetra

O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
great powerful master,

to glorify you,  
where should we start  
and where should we end?  
(We do not know the poet's art,  
by which he can describe things  
to his heart's content.)

O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
exalted divine master,

even if we could properly describe  
with selected words and perfect speech  
your achievements and your fame,  
how much of your greatness would we hide  
behind our false prestige and ego's pride?

O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
pure and gentle master,

one who really wants to give you praise  
must go on struggling with sincerity,  
give up this world s terrible race  
and reach the platform of purity.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
compassionate and kind master,

who traveled over this planet without any fear  
and woke up the sleeping, the blind, and the lame,  
giving the inexhaustible nectar of the Lord's holy name.  
Though lowborn, foolish, unqualified to hear,  
eternally we pray to you—please draw us near.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
dazzling unyielding master,

with the arrows of Vedic conclusion  
you challenged avidyā to fight,  
standing against him in battle, shining bright,  
you destroyed māyā’s illusion.

*O Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
*grave thoughtful master,*  
*your unfathomable deep mind,*  
*filled with Kṛṣṇa's unlimited ways*  
*of loving exchanges in the spiritual sky,*  
*reveals our darkness—and what is behind.*

O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
eternal, immortal master,

*your words can never be changed.*  
*What you speak—that is the truth.*  
*Somehow make us stay in your line.*  
*O Lord, please let that be arranged!*  
*O Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
*you blessed us all to be strong,*  
*but in your absence so much went wrong.*  
*Now let us take up the weapons*  
*that you have prepared,*  
*let us conquer the senses,*  
*lust, anger, and greed,*  
*for without winning the battle, how can we meet?*

(written by Aṣṭaratha dāsa)

*Your servants at Nava Jiyada-nṛsiṁha-kṣetra (West Germany),*

## Nagpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

We would like to offer you our words of appreciation for kindly having given us the opportunity to serve Lord Kṛṣṇa. In the early seventies you expressed your desire to open an ISKCON center in Nagpur. But all of us serving here are very, very unqualified, materially and spiritually as well. Most of us have come from remote, poor Indian villages and are bereft of an proper education, culture, or civilized way of life. Despite all these discrepancies in our character, by your causeless mercy we have been entrusted to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness in central India on our own. This is the most transcendental and sublime altruistic activity. Due to our unclean and rough characters, we have so far proved ourselves failures in terms of discharging devotional service. Our efforts to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness are like those of an ambitious baby who wants to touch the moon with his little hands.

Being aware of our weaknesses, and this awareness being our only qualification, we fall at your lotus feet, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and pray to you on this most auspicious anniversary of your appearance: Please give us the strength and intelligence so that we may be able to spread the message of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu. Sometimes, of course, we consider ourselves fortunate enough because, by the will of Providence, we are kept in touch with you through your sublime organization, ISKCON. We are even proud of having a grandfather like you who owns a beautiful house in which the whole world can be accommodated comfortably. Once again, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray at your merciful feet: Please be merciful toward all of us so that we may execute devotional service in the way we are supposed to. We beg to remain your humble servants.

*Your grandchildren in Nagpur, India,*

Rādhā-ramaṇa dāsa / Vīrabhadra dāsa / Svayaṁ-bhagavān dāsa / Vidura dāsa / Vijaya Govinda dāsa / Sudāmā dāsa / Vrajabhūmi dāsa / Pandharīnātha dāsa / Jagajjanī-devī dāsī

## New Delhi

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet—the lotus feet, the glorification of which has established thousands of fallen souls on the path back home, back to Godhead; the lotus feet, the dust from which can empower a suffering *jīva* to swim ashore from this ocean of nescience, which is otherwise impossible to cross over; the lotus feet which, when served, can make *Kṛṣṇa-prema* readily available, even to ordinary people.

We have been trying to follow your instructions, and time is passing by. We cannot claim to have achieved much, but the thought of what we would otherwise have been—if we had not held on to your lotus feet—is a dreadful thought. So many years without you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, would have landed us in situations more horrible that we could even imagine. Now, new children, suffering from the scorching and ever-increasing heat of *māyā,* are arriving to take shelter under your protection—the divine umbrella of ISKCON. Please give us the potency and intelligence by which we can not only accommodate them but also show them what we have tried to see under your divine guidance, which is available to everyone in your books.

Kali-yuga is advancing, and circumstances are changing very fast. Scenes are changing every moment, as is destined, but we *jīvas* are your eternal servants. The realization that we are Kṛṣṇa' s eternal servants can dawn on us only if we serve you eternally. The changing times and circumstances have nothing much to do with our relationship with Your Divine Grace. That relationship is eternal, above all mundane considerations, and it is our only hope in this *saṁsāra,* this ocean of birth and death, this vast forest fire.

The small and insignificant difficulties that we in ISKCON face from time to time are just like small waves that have no impact on the gigantic ISKCON ship, which goes on and on, unperturbed.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please remain next to us always so that we may not deviate from the course laid down by Your Divine Grace—the course that we have decided to follow.

Almost all your children were youthful when they came to you. They are now getting older, but this does not matter as long as they feel your divine presence and remain determined to carry out your orders till their last breaths. Please bless your progeny with the strength to follow your example.

*Your fallen servants,*

Rādhā-ramaṇa dāsa / Anaṅga-mañjarī-devī dāsī and your grandchildren at ISKCON New Delhi

## New Gokula

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You write, "We hope, therefore, that people will derive the greatest benefit by studying *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* as we have presented it here, and if even one man becomes a pure devotee of the Lord, we shall consider our attempt a success." *(Bhagavad-gītā,* Preface)

From this one statement, we can understand how rare it is to dedicate oneself not only for one year, or ten years, or twenty years, but for an entire lifetime. Dedication to your mission in spite of all obstacles and disturbances is a path followed by those sincere in heart.

As one continues to chant the *mahā-mantra,* the dirt within the heart begins to loosen and give way and rise to the surface. The contaminations within become exposed, and we are forced to cast off the impurities, as one does while making ghee. Lust, greed, anger, avarice, jealousy, fault-finding, pride, envy, and the desire to be the controller, the proprietor, the enjoyer, or the dearmost friend are a few of the hurdles to jump in the race we all have to run. Every one of us will be tested, either now or ten years from now or thirty years from now, whatever time the Lord has allotted for us. The test is the same for everyone. As Confucius says, "Our greatest glory is not in never falling but in rising every time we fall."

Studying the qualities and characteristics and behavior of a pure devotee, we bow our heads in shame to see how we fall short of the mark. But by your mercy and kindness, and by the mercy and kindness found in the hearts of your disciples, we feel hope and renewed life in our attempt to become pure.

Preaching by fear causes disharmony, but preaching by love causes harmony. We pray that we can work together here at New Gokula-dhāma in a cooperative spirit of harmony. We pray that we may always see the good side of our Godbrothers and Godsisters instead of focusing on their faults. By this mentality we become like the bees going for honey. We pray that we can dedicate the rest of our lives to following your perfect plan of simple living and high thinking. We pray that we can always remember Kṛṣṇa and never forget Him and help others to do the same. Here at New Gokula we pray that we can show our love for you by how well we can cooperate together.

*parama-karuniko na bhavat-parah*  
*parama-socyatamo na ca mat-parah*  
*iti vicintya hare mayi pamare*  
*yad ucitam yadundtha tad dcara*

**"O Lord, no one is more merciful than You, and no one is more pathetic than I. I am very lowly and fallen. O Lord Yadunātha, please reflect on my case and do to me whatever is appropriate."**

*Your servants at New Gokula-dhāma, home of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-mohana (Vancouver, Canada),*

## New Govardhana

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai Śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, all glories to you, who have awakened us to our real responsibility in this life: to develop our love for the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

All glories to you, who have alerted us to the real facts of life: birth, death, old age, and disease.

It is our great fortune that after taking *sannyāsa,* you, acting as the instrument of the Supreme Lord, were able to profusely deliver the right medicine to one and all, the nectarean cooling rains to extinguish the blazing fire of material existence: Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

Our only hope is that we can continue to chant this wonderful *mahā-mantra,* which is so easy to do and which gives such miraculous effects, but which can only be done in a humble frame of mind. The real key is to beg forgiveness from the Vaiṣṇavas for the offense of criticizing them.

It is only for the benefit of others that you endeavored so hard in your most precious years to establish a society to give shelter to the hopeless *jīvas.* What a selfless sacrifice! But please protect us from the inevitable danger of living and preaching with other Vaiṣṇavas, the danger of Vaiṣṇava *aparādha.*

*mahat-sevāṁ dvāram āhur vimuktes*  
*tamo-dvāraṁ yoṣitāṁ saṅgi-saṅgam*  
*mahāntas te sama-cittāḥ praśāntā*  
*vimanyavaḥ suhṛdaḥ sādhavo ye*  
*(Bhāg. 5.5.2)*

Without the blessings of the devotees, how can we chant the holy name with a clean heart, free from disturbance?

How many times did you reveal that the secret of success is simply trying to please the spiritual master?

When will we become intelligent disciples and give up trying to do what we want and simply try to understand this simple thing: *yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo / yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto 'pi—*there is absolutely no question of any advancement until one surrenders to the *guru* and tries to understand and fulfill his desire at every moment?

Why are we wasting our years, languishing through the days of eating and sleeping, when all the while so many are suffering for want of your mercy, which we are supposed to give?

O great lion among preachers, you could accuse great world leaders of being nothing but bloodsuckers, and yet still they would offer you respect and appreciate your goodwill!

It is only due to the compassion of the greatest Vaiṣṇava that we in this world are still living despite the sins and sufferings that are being perpetuated.

Haridāsa Ṭhākura's compassion for those who were beating him to death rendered the Sudarśana *cakra* of Lord Caitanya powerless and induced Him to personally take the beating Himself.

It was only due to the compassionate entreaties of Advaita Ācārya that Lord Caitanya was moved to appear. And only the mercy of Lord Nityānanda was able to save Jagāi and Mādhāi from execution and make them the Lord's pure associates.

We have committed countless heinous sins to deserve the wrath of the Supreme Lord, but only due to your shelter are we still able to continue to chant the holy name.

O most worshipable one, you came from Vaikuṇṭha and left again in a flash. We are living on borrowed time. There is no need to stay here except to fulfill your mission. Please let our thoughts, words, and breaths be for that purpose only, and please cut asunder our hopelessly deep attachments, which have buried us in this world.

*tāndera caraṇa-sevi-bhakta-sane vāsa*  
*janame janame hoy ei abhilāṣ*

*Your servants at New Govardhana (Murwillumbhah, Australia),*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## New Māyāpura

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

In the *śāstras* it is said that no one can spread the message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness unless empowered by Kṛṣṇa Himself through the *paramparā.* In the same way, a disciple requires the spiritual master's mercy to glorify him and his innumerable qualities. Otherwise, how is it possible for a conditioned soul to recognize and truly appreciate the qualities of a pure Vaiṣṇava like you, Śrīla Prabhupāda?

Your presence on our planet can be compared to the sun shedding its radiance all over the universe, or to the Supersoul penetrating into everyone's heart, or to the air pervading everything existing inside and outside.

You are the representative of the Vaiṣṇava *sampradāya* coming directly from Śrī Caitanya, and just like Him, you have always fought victoriously against Māyāvādī philosophy and atheism, which are prevailing in our age.

As the person *bhāgavata,* the perfect representative of the *Bhāgavatam,* you have constantly and brilliantly vanquished, without fail, Kali's soldiers in the disguise of scientists, politicians, and others.

With much skill you have infused in everyone's heart the divine message according to time, place and personalities. But you gave to all, without exception, the universal remedy: the chanting of Kṛṣṇa's holy names, which takes one back to the Lord in His eternal kingdom.

Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī glorifies Lord Caitanya as the most magnanimous *avatāra,* one who gave what nobody else had ever given before—pure love for Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. And you gave us, in the manner of Lord Caitanya, this matchless gift, without any discrimination.

Our matchless gift here in France is New Māyāpura. You wanted this place to be the example of an ideal and perfect society, and also a shelter for all the souls exhausted by material existence. Our sincere desire, now, is to develop this wonderful project, with your mercy.

In the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* Kṛṣṇadāsa Kavirāja Gosvāmī tells how he was hesitant to reveal the confidential pastimes of Lord Caitanya to the world because the atheists would misinterpret them and soil them. But, at the same time, he thought that the sincere devotees would appreciate them. And thus he wrote of the Lord's *antya-līlā.*

You also, Śrīla Prabhupāda, boldly revealed—through the *Kṛṣṇa* book and the *Nectar of Devotion—*the most sublime and intimate spiritual subjects.

You often said that we were Lord Caitanya's soldiers, but you are our general. You wanted us to preach, but you will always be the best preacher. You gave so much importance to your books—both publication and distribution—and you have always been the one who took the greatest pleasure in reading them and distributing them.

You are the perfect Vaiṣṇava whom everyone should follow and serve.

Here in New Māyāpura-dhāma we don't want our mind to be diverted in any way from your service. We want to follow your instructions. As you once said, "Our movement exists to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa. In this way we can always be happy in any circumstance. Never leave the shelter of this movement; never leave ISKCON."

With these instructions carved in our hearts, and with your mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we will gradually spread the *yuga-dharma* in this land.

*Your humble servants in New Māyāpura (Valencay, France),*

## New Nandagrām

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

You are the embodiment of Lord Caitanya's preaching potency, which, during your stay on this planet, was manifest in unlimited numbers of ways.

Your preaching was indiscriminate: it fell like the rain, which falls everywhere. Men, women, and children of all races had the opportunity to hear about Kṛṣṇa from your lotus mouth. Rich or poor, you never turned anyone away who was willing to listen.

Here are some of the inconceivable qualities you manifested while spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness throughout the world: your complete detachment towards things of no value in propagating Kṛṣṇa consciousness; your sharp eyes for detail and precision in carrying out devotional service, whether it be Deity worship or saving a few *paisā;* your ability to set up an organization such as ISKCON in such a manner that it would hold together and continue to expand after your departure.

Above all, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you deeply cared for all the conditioned souls, particularly those squandering away their valuable human life. You showed us the real meaning of selflessness. You alone, Śrīla Prabhupāda, unmitigatedly laid the foundation stones for the house in which the whole world could live. Oh, but for a fraction of your potency!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us strength.

*Your unworthy servants at New Nandagrām Farm (Bambra, Australia),*

## New Rādhā-kuṇḍa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,Please accept our humble obeisances.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

"I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvatī Gosvāmī. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanyadeva and delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism."

Although you never visited here, you gave this place the name New Rādhā-kunda because you saw a picture of it showing the house surrounded by a nice garden and a small lake.

For us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are always here because we are trying to serve you by producing your books (this is the headquarters of the North European BBT), by worshiping Śrī Śrī Gāndharvikā-Giridhārī and Gaura-Nitāi, by distributing your books, and by growing flowers and vegetables.

You are very dear to Kṛṣṇa because of your extraordinary service attitude, expressed in your unflinching determination to fulfill the most important missionary work in this material world, where everyone is a rascal and a demon. Only by your causeless mercy and fixed determination have we surrendered to Kṛṣṇa.

Sometimes we ask ourselves, "What would have happened if we had not joined this movement? What would we be doing now out there?" We would just be some nonsensical people without any goal in life.

You have changed the hearts of millions—whether they be your disciples or granddisciples, friends or life members of this movement, or persons who have gotten one of your books, tasted *prasādam,* and chanted the holy name. Indeed, on the whole of this planet earth it is hard to find someone who has not come into contact with your movement. This is the result of your work. Although not everyone is aware of how his heart has changed because of having performed some *ajñāta-sukrti* or *nāmābhāsa,* because the Supersoul has recognized their spiritual activities, their destiny has changed. From the *Bhagavad-gītā* we understand,

*nehābhikrama-nāśo ’sti*  
*pratyavāyo na vidyate*  
*sv-alpam apy asya dharmasya*  
*trāyate mahato bhayāt*

**"In this endeavor there is no loss or diminution, and a little advancement on this path can protect one from the most dangerous type of fear."**

You displayed your knowledge and expertise in so many kinds of activities, such as preaching, book production, temple construction, cooking, farming, etc. Your instructions and your perfect example will lead us all far into the future.

Because the moral codes, natural environment, and general health of the populace are becoming ruined nowadays, politicians and scientists are searching for solutions to these problems. We are proud to say that in your books there are already the most perfect solutions to these problems. These books are meant to bring about a revolution in the impious life of a misdirected civilization.

People are now blinded by material advancement and are showing hardly any interest in spiritual things. Without spiritual guidance, everything will be a failure. You once said, "A dog is running on four legs, and a man is running on four wheels. What is the difference?" The people of the world will remain in a helpless situation until they take to heart the spiritual knowledge you have presented in your books. Material advancement can become useful only if modern man is guided by your instructions.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are like a gardener tenderly taking care of our *bhakti-latā.* You are watching how the seeds are sprouting, you give water when required, you are pulling out the weeds, you give the plants sticks to hold on to, and you are taking care that the flowers are not stolen by thieves and that the plants are producing proper fruits. You are giving us shelter. You are taking care of our devotional life in all stages and situations. You have guided us by your instructions, and you have made the process of Kṛṣṇa consciousness very tasty and attractive. For all this, we thank you from the bottom of our hearts today.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, please establish Kṛṣṇa consciousness in our hearts.

Please bless us with the same kind of vigor in preaching that you have, so that we may become successful like you.

Our lives are dedicated to you.

*Your humble servants at New Rādhā-kuṇḍa (Korsnas Gard, Sweden).*

## New Ramaṇa Reti

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. By your causeless mercy and strong desire to save the conditioned souls, we are advancing in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are foolish, weak-hearted and disqualified in devotional service, but we are stumbling forward. We recently discussed the fact that it is possible for someone of no qualification to advance in Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and even develop a taste for devotional service, by the blessings and desires of one who is fixed in pure devotional service.

Whatever tests and obstacles we have successfully completed have been by your mercy. Other stumbling blocks lie ahead. Please make us fixed in your service and keep us there. Falling again and again in the dust of your louts feet in our weak condition, we pray for your service.

*Your servants in New Ramaṇa Reti (Alachua, Florida).*

## New Tālavana

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your wonderful service. We beg to engage eternally at your lotus feet and aspire only for your satisfaction. Your satisfaction is pleasing to the Supreme Lord because you have everyone's real welfare at heart. Therefore when you become satisfied, so does humanity and other species of life. If only we were more sincere and desirous to give the world your great message.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it is our desire to again try to devote our body, mind, and words to the service of your lotus feet. We know you must tire at our insincere efforts. Please allow us your mercy. Please allow us the shade of your lotus feet. We will try harder this year to devote all of our energy to fulfilling your mission. Please don't give up on us. We will correct our multitude of faults and work every day as need be.

Of course, you would love to see us grow up spiritually and become competent servants of our *paramparā.* This is our perfection. All the devotees are wanting this community, this *gurukula,* this preaching to be a success just to please you and Lord Kṛṣṇa and save our fallen fellow souls from repeated birth and death. Oh, how we wish we were more cooperative, transcendental, and truly Kṛṣṇa conscious! Therefore, every day we must practice fulfilling your orders and working for your pleasure so as to assure our success and the projects' perfection.

Again, we sincerely hope for a place at your lotus feet. Please wipe our hearts clean! Please do what is required for our hearts to melt in relation with you as our beloved preceptor. Your compassion knows no bounds, and we must be a transmitter of it. How can we possibly act righteously, forcefully, and lovingly enough to represent your teachings? At least we must not change the teaching. Even in the most difficult circumstances, we must present every teaching purely. At least we can do that. We will also work every day to manifest a true Kṛṣṇa conscious character ourselves and make the project and community a true manifestation of your vision.

Once again we beg to remain fully surrendered at your divine lotus feet.

*Your servants New Tālavana (Carriere, Mississippi).*

## New Vrajamaṇḍala

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, O master of the three worlds, you worked with such determination to present *nāma-saṅkīrtana* as the solution for all problems in this world. You traveled all around the planet many times to keep this movement moving, although you knew that doing this would decrease the duration of your life by years. You gave the understanding of real spiritual life to the Western world after five thousand years.

*namas te sārasvate deve*  
*gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-*  
*pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Only a *śakty-āveśa avatāra* could do such a huge amount of preaching in such a short time. You translated and wrote so many books. You never hid yourself away in seclusion but were always traveling, instructing, worshiping, lecturing, and answering letters. You are the personification of the practical mission of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, whose purpose is the same as Lord Kṛṣṇa's: *paritrāṇāya sādhūnāṁ vināśāya ca duṣkṛtām.*

We will surely find predictions in the scriptures about the coming of Your Divine Grace as time goes on—another source of inspiration to help us increase our faith and serve you better. Śrī Śukadeva Gosvāmī gives an indication in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (1.5.11):

*tad-vāg-visargo janatāgha-viplavo*  
*yasmin pratiślokam abaddhavaty api*  
*nāmāny anantasya yaśo 'ṅkitāni yat*  
*śṛnvanti gāyanti gṛṇanti sādhavaḥ*

**"On the other hand, that literature which is full of descriptions of the transcendental glories of the name, fame, form, pastimes, etc., of the unlimited Supreme Lord is a different creation, full of transcendental words directed toward bringing about a revolution in the impious lives of this world's misdirected civilization. Such transcendental literatures, even though imperfectly composed, are heard, sung, and accepted by purified men who are thoroughly honest."**

You said, "And if sinful people get away by being in distant countries where my personal preaching will not reach, joti *pāpī cārī dharma dura vesejana, moi senāpati-bhakta jāibe adhai:* I will send my devotee commander in chief, *senāpati-bhakta,* to deliver them. I will inundate the whole universe with pure love for Kṛṣṇa. I will not leave anyone to suffer or be in anxiety, moving or nonmoving beings."

Śrīla Prabhupāda-kī Jaya!

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please help us to always maintain the meditation that this is no favor to ISKCON we are doing by serving here, but that our meager service to you is a wonderful opportunity we have to assist in your mission. This mission is definitely a continuation of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu's pastimes, for those who have the eyes to see.

Hare Kṛṣṇa. Begging to remain your eternal servants,

*Your devotees at New Vrajamaṇḍala (Brihuega, Spain),*

## North Sydney

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Yours is a mission of mercy—to bring light to a benighted age. Divine knowledge, contained in an essential form in your Bhaktivedanta purports, is the only panacea and the instrument for redirecting a misguided civilization. Such knowledge is capable of providing a framework for the respiritualization of the entire human society. Material knowledge and material endeavors remain so many zeroes without the prime integer, Kṛṣṇa.

You have powerfully, creatively, humbly, and wonderfully taught divine knowledge and also acted as the foremost modern exemplar of that *divya-jñāna.*

Your personal example of complete surrender to and blissful contemplative meditation on your beloved Lord Govinda demonstrates the perfect synthesis of work and devotion implicit in the name "Bhaktivedanta."

Let us sincerely serve and follow you without being guilty of either imitation or deviation.

Let us continue to be enthused, entranced, and humbled by the wonderful activities that you have exhibited (your *līlāmṛta*) and remain dedicated to pushing on your mission in whatever way possible, to the best of our abilities, until our last breath.

We beg to remain your servants in the service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gokulānanda.

(written by Vṛṣa dāsa)
