## Nova Gokula

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai Śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

We offer our most humble obeisances unto your lotus feet.

In this material world the conditioned souls have so many problems to be solved. The demands of the senses and of the mind, although illusory, compel the conditioned souls to work very hard in this material world, so that they hardly find time to be engaged in pure devotional service to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. In your purport to *Bhagavad-gītā* 2.29, you write," \[People\] have very little time to understand the question of self-understanding, even though it is a fact that without this self-understanding all activities result in ultimate defeat in the struggle for existence. Perhaps they have no idea that one must think of the soul, and thus make a solution to the material miseries."

In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* it is stressed that if one fails to develop a taste for devotional service, even though one may perform his material activities perfectly, his life is useless *(śrama eva hi kevalam).*

On this most auspicious day of your appearance, we beg you to give us strength to penetrate the innermost core of the conditioned souls' hearts and there plant your transcendental words and topics about Kṛṣṇa—topics that nobody ever revealed in such a magnificent way as you did. Topics about Kṛṣṇa, when spoken by your lotus mouth, make the conditioned souls immediately reject the materialism of eating, sleeping, mating, and defending.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, if there is any benediction that you would like to give us, then help us influence the materially attached persons so that they will feel some attraction to read your books and thus understand the spiritual reality behind this material illusion.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you endeavored so much to give us devotional service and Kṛṣṇa consciousness. You left Vṛndāvana and Bhārata-varṣa when you were 70 years old and came to such a crazy and depressed place as this Western world. Now please empower us so that all your endeavors will fructify. When you arrived in America you revealed your mind to Kṛṣṇa and declared:

*taba kṛpā ha'le mor kathā śuddha habe*  
*śuniyā sabāra śoka duḥkha je ghucibe*

**"Only by your causeless mercy will my words become pure. I am sure that when this transcendental message penetrates their hearts, they will certainly feel engladdened and thus become liberated from all unhappy conditions of life."**

You were sure about that. So now we must be convinced as well. Nevertheless, convinced or not, we pray to you today to purify our hearts and allow us to understand that our main mission is to make the whole world swim in the ocean of the nectar of your words. In this way, the fire of millions and millions of material desires in the hearts of the conditioned souls will be extinguished.

*saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka-*  
*trāṇāya kāruṇya-ghanāghanatvam*  
*prāptasya kalyāṇa-guṇārṇavasya*  
*vande guroḥ śrī-caraṇāravindam*  
*he guro jñāna-da dīna-bandhu*  
*svānanda-dātā karuṇaika-sindho*  
*vṛndāvanāsīna hitāvatāra*  
*prasīda rādhā-praṇaya pracāra*

Due to your desire to impart knowledge to the fallen souls, due to compassion towards them, you descended from Goloka-Vṛndāvana, and now you are giving us our own bliss *(svānanda-dātā).* Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your goodness is too big to be expressed in mere words. Just allow us to serve in your ISKCON.

*Your humble servants from Nova Gokula (Pindamonhangaba, Brazil),*

## Ottawa

Rejoice! Rejoice, my dear Godbrothers!  
Ninety-three years today,  
His descent on the earth, unlike any other,  
teaches the way to pray—

Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare,  
Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare.

*Who could imagine our unfathomed good fortune,*  
*to 've been included in Bhaktivinoda s prophecy:*  
*Foreign Vaiṣṇavas in Māyāpura-dhāma,*  
*Chanting "Jaya Śacī-nandana!" dancing in ecstasy.*  
*O Prabhupāda, you are that empowered personality*  
*Whose determination and devotion brought us there,*  
*Your ISKCON is spreading successfully,*  
In spite of māyā’s glare.  
*"If you, can't expand, at least maintain."*  
*This is your command to us.*  
*But in order to maintain, we have to retain*  
A humble mood of love and trust!  
*Fanaticism breeds disunity,*  
*Our personal motives have shown,*  
*O Śrīla Prabhupāda! Give us the vision to see,*  
*So in the end, in Kṛṣṇa, we will have grown.*  
*Your divine instructions are like a razor's edge:*  
*One slip and we get cut.*  
*I've bled, I've floundered in keeping my pledge.*  
*I'm still grasping your dhotī to get out of māyā’s rut,*  
*I won t attempt a shallow "Thank you,"*  
*Nor a pretentious offering of a flower.*  
*Please tame my mad mind, which is like a zoo,*  
*So I may preach your mission with spiritual power.*

*Your humble servants at Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi's Mandira in Ottawa, Canada,*

Lakṣmī-nātha dāsa / Gaura dāsa / Śaṅkara dāsa / Vimala dāsa / Mahārāja Nanda dāsa / Lakṣmaṇā-devī dāsī / Śānti-devī dāsī

**Children**

Nārada / Sītā / Pañca-tattva / Uddhava

## Panama

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

You instructed us that we are not these material bodies but that we are spirit souls, and you are actively engaged in preaching the message of *Bhagavad-gīta.* You have revived within our hearts, through the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* love of God.

The means to execute your desire and will is contained within your books. You have said that everything you wanted to say is in your books. The more we hear and listen to you, the more we will learn how to please you.

The living entities are wandering in this material world through different species of life from time immemorial, but only due to the mercy of Kṛṣṇa and the spiritual master does the spirit soul get the seed of devotional service. It is true that simply by following your instructions, everything is accomplished.

By the appearance of Your Divine Grace, all darkness is dissipated, ignorance is destroyed, and the living entity realizes his constitutional position as the part and parcel and servant of Kṛṣṇa.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. On this auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we beg to reaffirm our faith in your instructions, and we seek your special mercy that we may fully surrender to you.

*Your servants at the Panama Mandira,*

Rāmāi dāsa / Haryānanda dāsa / Rājendra dāsa / Ekadaśī dāsa / Giri-govardhana dāsa / Śuddha-bhakta dāsa / Kṛṣṇa Gopāla dāsa / Svayambhuva dāsa / Māyeśa dāsa / Ekarāja dāsa / Kali dāsa / Madana Gopāla-devī dāsī / Śrī Ramā-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇa-preṣṭha-devī dāsī / Govinda-priyā-devī dāsī / Yamunā-jīvana-devī dāsī

## Pandharpur

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to the *guru-paramparā*! All glories to Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu!

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the proprietor of the ocean of love of Godhead and the mercy delivered by Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. But unfortunately, too often we forget our constitutional position of servitor-ship and, covered by illusion, fight among ourselves. Due to illusion, we come under the influence of false pride and begin thinking in terms of "my temple," "my zone," "my disciples," "my projects," etc. Instead of thinking in terms of "me" and "mine," we should remember that everything in ISKCON is your property. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have expanded yourself in the form of ISKCON. Our bodies, minds, words—everything we possess is your property. Please engage us in your transcendental, pure, loving, and unmotivated service, by which we can be purified and become reliable servants for the movement of Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Please give us enough intelligence and memory so that we may not forget your orders and instructions, and so that we may not become sentimental logicians.

Please fix our minds always at your lotus feet so that we may be able to serve our Guru Mahārājas. Our spiritual masters are authorized representatives of your self and Lord Kṛṣṇa. So, on this auspicious anniversary of your appearance, we are just begging at your lotus feet. Please forgive us for all our offenses.

*Your granddisciples at the Hare Krishna Ashram in Pandharpur, India.*

## Paris

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. On the auspicious day of your appearance, we are naturally reminded of how much our life has been saved by your merciful glance upon us and how much we are depending on your guidance for our ongoing march out of the grips of *māyā.*

You are our guide and savior.

You are the only light shining in the darkness of ignorance.

You are our only well-wisher.

You are the purest of the pure.

You are *dharma* personified.

You are the most charitable and the most magnanimous.

The conclusion of the teachings of the greatest sages of all times is flowing through your mouth as an endless river of nectar.

The shade of your lotus feet is most refreshing.

The mission you invested your movement with is a burden of love we wish to carry on our heads life after life.

Cooperation with your devotees is our first regulation.

Pleasing you by preaching Lord Gaurāṅga's message is our only solace.

Without your shelter, we are utterly lost. Please keep us engaged in your loving service. Please give us the strength to sustain our dedication. Please accept us as your eternal servants. We are yours forever. Please have mercy and bless us with unalloyed devotion.

*Your servants at the Paris temple (Noisy le Grand),*

## Perth

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet on the occasion of your appearance day.

As aspiring followers of Your Divine Grace, on this most most auspicious day—as well as throughout the whole year—we genuinely desire to offer you so much. However, our efforts to push on the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement here in Perth, Australia, are but a very humble contribution, a token offering, to your great preaching mission.

Of course, by way of your merciful appearance in this world, your offering to us is unlimited. We may not have had your intimate personal association. We may not have heard the divine message of Kṛṣṇa consciousness directly from your lotus mouth. We may not have received direct correction and instruction from you to effectively maximize our spiritual progress. For just a few short years, in just a few precious moments, you gave personal attention to just a few fortunate souls.

Still, our great fortune is that your universal offering is all-pervading. The transcendental literatures you composed disseminate absolute instructions applicable to one and all. Your beloved servants, even in your absence, work so sincerely to project your mission all over the world. In a sublime, transcendental way, your loving shelter is experienced by even those, like ourselves, who render just a little service.

Certainly, by Kṛṣṇa's supreme grace, in response to the offering of love of his pure devotee, just as we have been blessed, so also many fortunate persons are now receiving Kṛṣṇa consciousness in many far-off, distant places.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue to bless us so that we may act with all good intelligence in accordance with your desire. Please bless us so that we may gain strength to overcome all weaknesses of heart. And please bless us so that we may push on regardless of any temporary so-called setbacks.

*Your servants at ISKCON Perth, Australia,*

Vaṅka-vihārī dāsa / Indra Bhaṭṭa dāsa / Śrī Rāma dāsa / Bhakta Mark / Bhakta Steve / Śucirāṇī-devī dāsī / Viṣṇupriyā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Norlinna

## Philadelphia

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

With all our hearts we would like to glorify you with suitable praise on this most auspicious day. But in what manner can we give expression to our love for you? Perhaps this one statement will suffice:

O Jagat-guru, O Paramahaṁsa Ṭhākura, O Vaiṣṇava Gosvāmī, O Mahā-mahā-bhāgavata, Śrīla Prabhupāda—you saved us!

This statement expresses our realization of your exalted position, our fallen state, and your causeless mercy. While we can't fully comprehend your highly exalted, transcendental stature, we have begun to appreciate it. We have begun to glimpse some of your transcendental qualities. Rarely has the world seen a pure devotee such as you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Shall we not be bold and say, in fact, that never has it seen the likes of your purity and magnanimity?

Gifted with the ability to free the lowest, most ignorant, helpless living entities from the insurmountable control of the modes of material nature, you traveled far, delivering freely the benediction of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Your potency is such, your intense desire to relieve the world of the all-encompassing fire of *saṁsāra* is so strong, that even in your physical absence countless souls are relishing the nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness distributed by you through your disciples and granddisciples. What a wonder, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Śrīla Rūpa Gosvāmī describes Lord Caitanya as the most magnanimous incarnation of Kṛṣṇa, because without discrimination He gave the valuable gem of Kṛṣṇa consciousness to everyone. But, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know you to be even more magnanimous. This is the special benediction of the disciplic succession. You have described how this is possible in your purport to *Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Madhya-līlā* 16.65: "By the grace of Viṣṇu, a Vaiṣṇava can render better service than Viṣṇu: that is the special prerogative of a Vaiṣṇava."

Your pure desire to relieve Lord Caitanya of His anxiety to save the conditioned souls shows how much you are filled with pure and transcendental compassion and magnanimity. Your being overflows with compassion, and we find ourselves caught in its waves. We become swept up in your desire to save the conditioned souls. Truly you have transferred your desire to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness to our hearts, and we want to render you service by helping you deliver the living entities in this universe. You have made us eternally happy in Kṛṣṇa consciousness; we cannot help but want to share this blissful realm with others.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we want to worship you by helping the world understand your glorious position as Kṛṣṇa's dearest servitor. We want them to experience the shelter of your lotus feet, for there is no other shelter in the three worlds.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, who are so dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa and Lord Caitanya, we need your help to fulfill this desire. We need spiritual strength, scriptural vision, youthful enthusiasm—we need your guidance. Material nature seems invincible. We have tested our strength against hers, and we have become frightened. We must realize that we will be spared her onslaught only by seriously taking to heart your instructions.

You have equipped us well to combat Māyā, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You've given us an ecstatic daily morning festival, replete with song and dance, rigorous philosophy, and Kṛṣṇa's *prasādam.* You've given us four regulative principles as a protective shield. You 've given us Kṛṣṇa's holy names! You've given us unlimited service to do for Kṛṣṇa. And you've emphatically told us to just stick tightly to this *sādhana* program to guarantee our spiritual safety.

Our spiritual lives have been formed by your mercy and careful guidance. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please continue to sustain our Kṛṣṇa conscious lives. Please give us intelligence to follow our daily *sādhana.* Please let us eternally serve your lotus feet.

(written by Prāṇadā-devī dāsī)

*Your servants at ISKCON Philadelphia,*

## The Philippines

Our ever-dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at the dust of your lotus feet.

Lord Caitanya predicted, "My holy name will be heard in every town and village." This prophecy you have fulfilled, for you have brought to us the teachings of Caitanya Mahāprabhu through your faithful devotees who have been sincerely serving you until now. Through their valuable association and instructions, we were able to understand your books. Please bestow your blessings upon us so that we may be able to do just a little bit of service to please you. Empower us to distribute your timeless books to all these poor souls so that they will become happy, too. This is the only way in which we can attempt to repay you for saving us from the darkest region of hellish life.

Please continue showering your compassion upon us through your dearmost disciples, for they are your mercy personified.

*Your servants at ISKCON, the Philippines,*

## Poland

Please accept our most respectful and humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this Vyāsa-pūjā day, we are supposed to offer you something. But it is you who have given us everything, the most valuable jewel and the only real thing in this world: Kṛṣṇa consciousness, along with this movement, which gives us the opportunity to become devotees of Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our real and only benefactor, and you are our only real protector, because your protection is eternal. You have given us your books, which contain your divine instructions. These are meant for helping us to develop love of Godhead and finally to go back home, back to Kṛṣṇa. Because of these books, we and all other souls who take shelter of them are eternally protected from the stringent laws of material nature and repeated birth and death. We are eternally indebted to you, and we would like to show our gratitude by becoming tools for your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

Today we have something special to offer you: we have completed publishing all your books in the Polish language. You are always stressing the importance of book production and distribution. We know that these are your heart and soul. So now we can overflood the people of Poland with your books. Once, when our spiritual master, Harikeśa Swami, told you about how he was distributing many books in Poland, you predicted that Kṛṣṇa consciousness would manifest very wonderfully there. And we see that your prediction is coming true. So many people—hundreds and hundreds—are coming for programs and visiting our farm. Now that we have a full set of your books in Polish, the real preaching will start.

We also want to make a place where people can come and see how we are practically applying this philosophy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in our lives. So we have our farm project, where we are showing people how to live peacefully and be happy protecting the cows and bulls and chanting Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Recently we rented a new temple in Warsaw, where, finally, all the devotees and newcomers can live together and redouble their efforts to spread this movement.

All in all, we are witnessing how you are showering great mercy upon our country, and we hope that we will be able to take full advantage by becoming nice devotees ourselves and preaching very nicely. In this way we will glorify you greatly and prove that you are the empowered representative of Kṛṣṇa who came to save all the fallen, conditioned souls in this world.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know that the spread of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in Poland is simply a manifestation of your causeless mercy. Thank you very much again and again.

*Your grateful granddisciples in the Polish yātrā.*

## Port Harcourt

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Kindly accept our humble obeisances. All glories to you! All glories to Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura! All glories to Śrī Guru and Gaurāṅga!

The only unfortunate thing is that we never knew you in person. If we had, perhaps our devotional life would be more free from *anarthas.* We only know you through your countless faultless books, which reveal the nature of the Absolute Truth and teach the process to attain the ultimate realization of that truth, pure love for Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. If, on the other hand, we *had* had intimate association with you, that might have been the cause of our spiritual suicide due to offenses. It is therefore a perfect arrangement of Kṛṣṇa that we should not have known you in person but through you instructions, which our Gurudevas have fully assured us are leading us to the ultimate perfection in life.

If we may ask, Why is it so difficult to understand your instructions? Why is there a falling preaching standard? Why is Māyā hitting so hard? Why is there disintegration within the movement you single-handedly established? Is it due to our offenses or our improperly following your instructions, or are we minimizing your instructions? Please answer these questions. We know you can, since, as it is said, "Vaiṣṇavas never die." They leave a situation in which service to Kṛṣṇa can expand. Therefore please reveal yourself to us and tell us where we have gone wrong. Rectification is never too late.

*Your servants in Port Harcourt, Nigeria,*

Mahānanda dāsa / Murāri Gupta dāsa / Ṛkṣarāja dāsa / Uttamaśloka dāsa / Padmanābha dāsa / Śeṣa Nāga dāsa / Viṣṇu dāsa / Bhakta Kelvin / Bhakta Johnson / Bhakta Nwachukwu / Bhakta Earnest / Bhakta Kingsley / Bhakta Emmanuel / Revatī-devī dāsī / Gāndhārī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Elizabeth / Bhaktin Beatrice / Bhaktin Fansat

## Puerto Rico

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

How can we attempt to glorify one who is so great? Of course, throughout the year we have been making some attempt (Oh, how inadequate!) to carry out this vital service of your glorification by means of distributing your transcendental message via your books and the basic programs of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, as you have instructed. *Harināma-saṅkīrtana* has been nicely received in the busy streets. And this is all the mercy of Your Divine Grace, the representative of Lord Caitanyadeva who has come to the Western world with His life-saving mission. Now the day has come: the all-auspicious anniversary of your divine appearance in this mortal world. And now it is our natural duty to speak a few words in your glorification. So we first of all feel moved to beg that you may accept this very neophyte attempt.

One simple fact of your glory, and one that easily "hits home," is your wonderful influence all over the world. Your ISKCON society is itself thrilled and is inspiring many persons internationally due to the amazing courage and resulting victories of the Soviet devotees. You were, after all, in Russia for an extremely short time, and your activities appeared to be restricted by the forces of *māyā.* Yet your influence is still growing to this very day. Similarly, despite our own failings and inability to fully manifest your great mercy here in this section of the tropical Caribbean, and even though our island was never blessed by the direct touch of your lotus feet on her soil, nevertheless your presence, your sublime influence, has certainly taken root. We thus take heart that nothing is lacking. We have only but to surrender to your *vāṇī.* Then your glories will be more manifest for the ultimate benefit of all concerned, including ourselves.

Lord Caitanya performed the pastime of causing a great upsurge of *Kṛṣṇa-bhakti* everywhere in India by setting off a kind of chain reaction. By His divine contact people became divinely empowered to awaken *Kṛṣṇa-bhakti* in others and to empower other souls to do the same. Conditioned souls became relieved of all false designations and totally absorbed in the holy names. In the same way, Your Divine Grace has not at all withheld any of your mercy from those persons and places that did not directly get your *darśana.* The only qualification for any of us is our own sincere desire to *take* that mercy. Thus you have gloriously enabled this entire world to become solidly linked once again with the Supreme Personality of Godhead. And you will always be entitled to "royalties" of further glorification as people become intelligent enough to accept your mercy in the form of your instructions.

Although none of us are accomplished, we would like to attempt a touch of rhyme:

*These islands are quite beautiful*  
*In a material sort of way,*  
*But your knowledge, your books,*  
*Show real light of day,*  
*Not of this world*  
*But of the highest abode,*  
*The one from where you came,*  
*The one to us you showed.*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your pleasure we yearn to unlimitedly reveal your glories and Lord Caitanya's munificence in every town and village of this land. Let us worship and glorify you in this way, as one worships Mother Ganges by offering her water back to her. This is entirely appropriate, for any successes we could ever offer you are nothing more than your own wonders, your own glorious pastimes unfolding.

Please therefore accept this very inadequate offering and grant us your mercy so that we may improve in our glorification of Your Divine Grace throughout the coming year. All glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda!

(written by Raghupatiprāṇa dāsa and Prabhupāda dāsa)

*Your servants from Śrī Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma Mandira (Puerto Rico),*

## Rāḍhadeśa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet:

*naiṣāṁ matis tāvad urukramāṅghriṁ*  
*spṛśaty anarthāpagamo yad-arthaḥ*  
*mahīyasāṁ pāda-rajo-’bhiṣekaṁ*  
*niṣkiñcanānāṁ na vṛṇīta yāvat*

**Prahlāda Mahārāja states: "Unless they smear upon their bodies the dust of the lotus feet of a Vaiṣṇava completely free from material contamination, persons very much inclined toward materialistic life cannot be attached to the lotus feet of the Lord, who is glorified for His uncommon activities. Only by becoming Kṛṣṇa conscious and taking shelter of the lotus feet of the Lord in this way can one be freed of material contamination."** ***(Bhāg.*** **7.5.32)**

Your appearance in this world certainly blessed the earth with the dust of your lotus feet. Indeed wherever you traveled, the dust from your feet spread everywhere. Wherever your lotus feet touched, that place became sanctified and a place of pilgrimage *(tīrthī-kurvanti tīrthāni),* just as the Ganges purifies wherever it flows. The Ganges' course blesses both the land and its inhabitants, and anywhere along its banks is considered a place of pilgrimage. Similarly, by traveling and preaching the glories of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, you purified souls everywhere and established hundreds of temples as *tīrthas,* spiritual oases in a world overrun by materialism and atheism.

We residents of Rāḍhadeśa, however, feel deprived of a fortunate opportunity because during your physical presence you were not able to consecrate the land here with the impressions of your lotus feet. In this connection, "Rāḍhadeśa" generally refers to that part of Bengal where the Ganges doesn't flow. Rāḍhadeśa carries for us, therefore, a double bereavement: It is far across the world from the Ganges, and it is devoid of your holy footprints. This latter fact pains us most of all.

But on the other hand, "Rāḍhadeśa" also means "the country of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī," or "the place where devotional service under the protection of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī prevails." So how could this be possible in a locality which can only be termed as impious, having neither received the pure devotee nor been washed by the waters of the Ganges, and with a history only as a bastion during lordly feuds and as a battlefield for two world wars? What could possibly account for the remarkable spiritual phenomena that are occurring here?

Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura gives us insight into the matter:

*gaṅgāra parāśa hoile paścāte pāvan*  
*darśane pavitra koro ei tomāra guṇ*

In order to take advantage of the Ganges, one must come in personal contact with it. And even then, only after repeated bathings can one attain sinlessness. However, just by the *darśana* of a pure devotee, that same effect is achieved.

While you were here amongst us, spiritual seekers came by tens and hundreds to hear your discourses, take your *prasādam,* and chant the holy name. Undoubtedly, any person who had the fortune to receive a few drops of the *caraṇāmṛta* directly from you acquired special benefit. But, to this very day, people still come to join, asking for you. Are they also deprived of the fortune of having your *darśana*?

Our experience tells us otherwise. Have we not witnessed that the self-same *bhāgavata-darśana* which nurtured your own disciples is just as available to all the unfortunate souls of this age through your *vāṇī* (your books, recordings, and instructions)? You told us that if someone even *touches* one of your books, he is eternally benefited; or if he reads one word, his life is perfect. How can anyone say, then, that direct approach to you is exclusive to only your initiated disciples? If that were so, how could thousands of disciples of fallen *gurus* continue in ISKCON despite so much heartbreak and disappointment? Actually, as leaders and senior members of the Society, we owe them (and you) deep apologies for not being up to the standard you expected of us. If we seriously reflect on our less-than-perfect record since your departure, and honestly evaluate our deficiencies, we will see that the very best we can offer future generations is the legacy of your instructions—assisting as monitors, not dominating as didactic pedants.

We must understand that association with you doesn't stop with your physical demise. It goes on eternally through your *vāṇī,* enshrined. "He reasons ill who says that Vaiṣṇavas die, when Thou art living still in sound...." Are you not eternally present? You made it very clear to us you would never die: "I live forever in my books." (You are similarly quoted in *Śrīla Prabhupāda -līlāmṛta,* vol. 6, pg. 97.) In your concluding words to the *Caitanya-caritāmṛta,* you state that *vapur,* the physical form, may be appreciated or not, but *vāṇī* continues forever. In fact, you go on to say that *vāṇī-sevā is* even more important than personal association. You yourself had not more than ten days' personal association with your spiritual master, yet no one could understand Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura better than you. Those who can comprehend the import of these points can understand you and the meaning of disciplic succession.

Such power was invested in you by Caitanya Mahāprabhu that you were able to ignite a "Hare Kṛṣṇa Explosion" that, like a supernova, has flung the atomic dust particles from your lotus feet far and wide—to every town and village across the globe. Sustained by its own self-generating potency, that transcendental dust continues to be proliferated by your devoted followers. Simply by contact with that dust, people receive the creeper of devotion.

Those who possess good sense will stay under the shelter of your lotus feet and beg to take that dust upon their heads. Then, in good faith, they become initiated and follow your instructions as their life's goal. By such surrender, they will certainly be delivered from all impurities in the near future and qualify themselves for the Supreme Lord's mercy

Those who are further advanced, however, forswear all sense of false prestige from materialistic position and roll in the transcendental dust of your feet again and again. Feeling ever-increasing invigoration, they become almost mad and contact everyone they meet with that sacred dust—just as intoxicated bees distribute the pollen of the lotus as they travel from flower to flower. Carrying the potent seeds of your books and instructions hither and thither, these devotees can inseminate thousands and millions of living entities with the *bhakti-latā.* By their determination and example, many more creepers will bud and thrive in the future.

Yet again, some may know it, or not know it—or even deny it—but anyone who, somehow or other, possesses such dust becomes an instrument in your mission to serve Caitanya Mahāprabhu. It is your seed—the dust from your feet—which is the cause of the spiritual rebirth for the fallen souls in this spiritually-averse and polluted age. That magical dust is full of all spiritual potencies, and it causes revolutionary things to happen in the lives of this world's impious and misdirected civilization.

Shortly after your disappearance, some of the dust from your feet settled here in the Ardennes. Cultivated by your faithful and perseverent followers in the Benelux Yātrā, it has taken the shape of this beautiful Rāḍhadeśa project, with expansive facilities for preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness. For ten years now, Rāḍhadeśa has provided a haven for conditioned souls to peacefully practice the principles of spiritual life, freeing them from material anxieties.

Although it sometimes has been a burden to maintain such a colossal edifice—especially in these troubled and turbulent times—you have shown us the cure-all for our problems: preaching. And in particular, your formula for success (which was also your unique qualification for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the West) was *yukta-vairāgya—*utilizing everything in the *saṅkīrtana-yajña.* Preaching therefore means to somehow or other distribute the dust of your lotus feet to everyone. By giving that dust to others, one (even inadvertently) becomes covered all over with it himself.

The castle here in Rāḍhadeśa is a recognized historical monument, and since the movement in Belgium is looked upon quite favorably, our community poses as a natural curiosity for the thousands of tourists who visit the region. By the inspiration of some of your dedicated servants, we have recently arranged for hundreds of bus groups to take guided tours of the large chateau—and simultaneously get a grand exposure to Kṛṣṇa consciousness. The program begins in the stately "Gold Room" with a slide show produced with the help of a renowned radio announcer who is a friend of the movement and likes to read your books. After nominally introducing the history of the castle, the narrator beckons viewers, "If you really want to know what's going on here, you have to come with us to India." And from then on, it's all straight philosophy. Generally, people are so impressed that they spontaneously applaud when the show is over. In such a mood of appreciation, they're ushered into the magnificent marble temple room to take darsan of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha. Before entering, they respectfully remove their shoes, and while admiring the splendor of the altar, they hear about the "Lords" of the castle and are taught to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa. On their way out, they sip *caraṇāmṛta* and offer donations for flowers, thus earning inestimable credits of piety. From the temple, they proceed upstairs to view the BBT art gallery, and then to the tower (six stories up) to get a birds-eye view of the grounds and gardens. After such intensive touring, they have the choice of either a full *prasādam* lunch at the restaurant, a munch at the snack bar, and / or an ice cream and soda at the sidewalk cafe. From the shop, they can also procure devotee-made honey, bread, and pastries to take home. Then, finally, as they leave, each family receives a *Kṛṣṇa-Bhakti* magazine. In this way, tens of thousands of happy "pilgrims" pass through Rāḍhadeśa's gates yearly and are thus indelibly blessed with transcendental benediction.

People come to Rāḍhadeśa as a mundane attraction. But you (in your *mūrti* form) and Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha are seated in the heart of Rāḍhadeśa as the cynosure, like the whorl inside a lotus. Actually, we can see that by so many transcendental devices the conditioned souls (like ourselves) are being tricked into accepting Kṛṣṇa consciousness. This is all going on by your mercy.

The new guest program promises to busily engage us all in your preaching mission, and carry a substantial portion of the financial weight for the project as well. All these developments are transforming tranquil Rāḍhadeśa into a beehive of activity. You always told us that if we sincerely tried to distribute Kṛṣṇa's glories. He would send the necessary funds and requirements to maintain such service. "Kṛṣṇa's no poor man." The more we endeavor in this direction, the more we are getting factual experience of the truth in your words.

It has been twelve long years since you were here to bless the world with the dust of your cooling lotus feet, and we are also feeling the burning pain of that separation. No more will we meet your piercing glance (which could free the soul), take your *mahā-mahā-prasādam* remnants (which could turn demons into devotees), or have our queries answered by you (which were the finality of conclusive truth). Nor are you there to resolve our seemingly irreconcilable disputes or mitigate our pressing difficulties. Lacking your physical presence, we can only beat our heads against the stone.

A saner proposal would be to simply roll in the dust of your lotus feet. Through your *vāṇī,* through your mission (ISKCON), and through your bona fide followers, you have left that dust for the generations of sincere spiritual aspirants to come. In fact, the dust from your feet can grant benedictions many times more than what has already been achieved by you personally.

As we go on faithfully, day by day and year by year, and as our hearts become more and more purified, we realize the great treasure that lies in the golden dust particles at your feet. By smearing ourselves fully with that dust, we can understand that your personal *darśana* is separated from us only by time. Actually, in the fire of service to you, Kṛṣṇa Himself manifests from within our hearts and within our activities; we realize our eternal connection with the Supreme Lord through you. This is our only solace.

Your worshipable Deities here in Rāḍhadeśa, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Gopīnātha, remind us of our separation from you and that the ultimate *prayojana* is to meet with you and Kṛṣṇa in the supreme spiritual abode, Goloka Vṛndāvana. Until that day comes when we are worthy to associate with you in our pure identities and serve you in our eternal spiritual forms, we pray to take advantage of the mercy that you have abundantly left us with in so many different ways.

Narottama dāsa Ṭhākura again instructs us in this regard:

*prati janme kori āsa caraṇera dhuli*  
*narottama koro doyā āpanara boli'*

**"I desire the dust of your holy feet in every birth that I may take. Please consider Narottama yours and be kind unto him."**

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please keep us in the dust of your lotus feet.

(written by Śrīla dāsa)

*Your aspiring servants in Rāḍhadeśa (Belgium),*

## Rio de Janeiro

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at yor lotus feet. And please accept this humble offering on this most glorious day—your Vyāsa-pūjā.

In the third canto of the Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Lord Kapiladeva graphically describes to His mother how the unborn child suffering in the womb prays to the Lord:

*yan-māyayoru-guṇa-karma-nibandhane 'smin*  
*sāṁsārike pathi caraṁs tad-abhiśramena*  
*naṣṭa-smṛtiḥ punar ayaṁ pravṛṇīta lokaṁ*  
*yuktyā kayā mahad-anugraham antareṇa*

**"The human soul further prays: The living entity is put under the influence of material nature and continues a hard struggle for existence on the path of repeated birth and death. This conditional life is due to his forgetfulness of his relationship with the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Therefore, without the Lord's mercy, how can he again engage in the transcendental loving service of the Lord?"**

The influence of the material world can be overcome only by the mercy of the spiritual master, whom one meets by the causeless mercy of the Supreme Lord. Bound by the shackles of the material energy, we pray to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for your mercy, which will free us.

In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* it is said that in the womb of our mothers we prayed to the Supreme Lord. In your purports you explain that we are in this condition due to forgetfulness of our relationship with the Lord, that this forgetfulness is due to the misuse of our free will, and that all this is done under the direction of the Supreme Lord. Only the spiritual master can take us out of this painful situation.

Now let us all pray for your mercy and for the mercy of Lord Caitanya so that we may be able to satisfy you by vigorously spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*Your servants in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

**Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## San Diego

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances on this, your divine appearance day.

At an initiation lecture delivered in Seattle, Washington, on October 20,1968, you said, "Many of our students are initiated. So some of our students are going to be initiated this evening. The initiation means the third stage of joining this movement. The first stage is *śraddhā,* a little faith. Just like our students are going in the market place. They are chanting, and many people are contributing some money. Somebody is purchasing our *Back to Godhead.* This is the beginning of faith: 'Oh, here is a nice movement. Let me cooperate.' *Ādau śraddhā.* Then if he becomes a little more interested, then he comes here into the class—'All right, let us see what these people are teaching, this Kṛṣṇa consciousness.' So they come. So that is the second stage. The first stage is automatic sympathy for this movement. The second stage is joining or cooperating or associating with our activities, just like you have kindly come here. You are hearing me. Similarly, if someone becomes more interested, or his faith is still further advanced, then he comes. That is the second stage. And the third stage is—*ādau śraddhā tataḥ sādhu-saṅgo 'tha bhajana-kriyā.* Now the initiation means the beginning of the activities, how one can develop Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the perfectional stage. That is called initiation."

In those words you outlined three stages of faith, and at each stage your followers in the International Society for Krishna Consciousness have a responsibility to represent you nicely.

The first step for the conditioned soul on the path to Kṛṣṇa consciousness is "a little faith." So we have a duty to go "to the marketplace" with the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mantra* and your transcendental literatures. That is the first point. Are we going out and giving the conditioned souls the chance to develop that little bit of faith? Are we going out to chant the holy name? Are we going out to give the conditioned souls the message of *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam*? Are we giving them *Back to Godhead*? Of course, this means someone has to print the books and magazines and get them into the hands of the devotees who will give them to the conditioned souls. Someone has to make the Balarāma *mṛdaṅgas.* But in simplest terms, we must ask ourselves, individually and collectively, Are we going out to give the conditioned souls the chance to develop that little bit of faith you spoke about?

And then we have to ask ourselves, Are we conducting ourselves in such a way that you would be pleased with us? Are we conducting the business of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness in such a way that we ourselves are not disturbing that little bit of faith in the conditioned soul. So much care and oversight is required to ensure that everything is being done properly and to the standard you set by your words and deeds. As spiritual master, you are our eternal guide, so please guide us in such a way that we do not spoil the "automatic sympathy" for this movement in the hearts of the conditioned souls.

Then there is something else to be concerned with on the stage of the initial little bit of faith, and that is the efforts of the enemies of genuine God consciousness to destroy any manifestation of such faith. The materialistic scientists are doing everything possible to prevent any little bit of faith from arising in the hearts of the people. Many envious officials, scholars, psychiatrists, lawyers, and journalists are doing everything they can to destroy the "automatic sympathy" the conditioned souls have for the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Therefore it is up to us to defend that little bit of faith and to defend the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement with utmost resolve and determination from any attack made upon it.

Now what about the second stage of faith, when a conditioned soul decides, "All right, let us see what these people are teaching, this Kṛṣṇa consciousness"? You say, "So they come." So where will they come? They will come to your temples, your farm communities, your restaurants. We therefore have a duty to ensure that your temples and other facilities are being nicely managed and protected, and increased. They will come to the class, to see the worship, to taste the *prasādam.* So all this must go on in the way you desired. Most important, are we going to be there, as you were, to give association, to preach, to take care of those who come?

And now the third stage, initiation. So someone develops that initial little bit of faith, they come and participate in the activities of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. What next? Will he or she desire to learn "how one can develop Kṛṣṇa consciousness to the perfectional stage"? Are we following our initiation vows? Are we strictly obeying the regulative principles, chanting our sixteen rounds of *japa,* attending the temple programs, and dedicating our lives to carrying out your orders? In other words, are we being good disciples of Your Divine Grace? If so, then yes, others will be inspired to take to this third stage of faith, initiation. But as you said, that is not the end, but the beginning.

Describing further stages, you said, *"Ruci* means they get a taste. Why these boys are going out, this chanting? They have got a taste. They have developed a taste. Otherwise, for nothing they are not wasting time. They are educated, they are grown up." You went on to say, *"Athāsakti.* When the taste is... then attachment. He cannot give it up. I receive so many letters. Some students—they could not cope with their Godbrothers. They go away. But they write, 'I cannot go. I cannot go.' He is captured."

We pray that we may remain captured, that in spite of all difficulties we may remain forever attached to your lotus feet, serving you eternally in your International Society for Krishna Consciousness.

(written by Drutakarmā dāsa)

*Your servants at New Govardhana (San Diego).*

## San Salvador

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai Śrī-gurave namaḥ*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda:

Let us offer our respectful obeisances at your lotus feet. O divine master and preceptor, you are the supreme adorable deity of our hearts.

On this auspicious occasion of Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, let us meditate on your unlimited transcendental qualities. Your glory is that you are intimately linked to the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and are thus submerged in the nectar of Goloka-Vṛndāvana. Seeing our utter lack of spiritual qualities, you descended to this dark world and approached us. Yet even though you walked amongst us, you always carried the ambrosia of the eternal *dhāma* within your heart, and thus you always exhibited the transcendental qualities of the spiritual world.

Your teachings, ordinarily unknown in the present demoniac civilization, are the external manifestation of the mercy of the supreme spiritual master, Lord Kṛṣṇa Himself. You personify love of God, as described by Śrī Prahlāda Mahārāja:

*naivodvije para-duratyaya-vaitaraṇyās*  
*tvad-vīrya-gāyana-mahāmṛta-magna-cittaḥ*  
*śoce tato vimukha-cetasa indriyārtha-*  
*māyā-sukhāya bharam udvahato vimūḍhān*

**"O best of the great personalities \[Lord Nṛsiṁha-deva\], I am not at all afraid of material existence, for wherever I stay I am fully absorbed in thoughts of Your glories and activities. My concern is only for the fools and rascals who are making elaborate plans for material happiness and maintaining their families, society, and countries. I am simply concerned with love for them."**

O dear Gurudeva, let us forever glorify your divine appearance in this mortal world! Please take us to the platform on which we can feel the transcendental happiness of the great souls, please fill us with compassion for the conditioned souls, and please help us continue pushing on the *saṅkīrtana* movement of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

*Your fallen but fortunate servants in San Salvador.*

## Santo Domingo

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
*On this, your day,*  
*we try to offer*  
*what we've done,*  
*and what we are about to do.*  
We also offer prayers  
*and praises to you,*  
*who purified the air we breathe,*  
*the sound, touch, food, and sight.*  
*Shamefully,*  
*We have not accomplished*  
*what we had promised,*  
*and without your causeless mercy,*  
*we won't be able to accomplish*  
*what we're about to offer.*  
*Furthermore,*  
*due to our lack of devotion,*  
*we only have what we have heard from you*  
*to offer back to you:*

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai Śrī-gurave namaḥ*

## Sao Paulo

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

As the moon gives the direction, showing the path in the darkness of the night, you are showing to us the path of perfection in this world of darkness. We cannot do anything without your mercy Because you know this, you are so merciful that you left with us many enlightened Vaiṣṇavas who are continuing your mission as you desire.

In this way, we are receiving the mercy of your great disciple Hridayānanda dāsa Goswami, and by trying to serve him, we are trying to serve you. So we pray to you that we may please him; then we will get your mercy.

We beg to remain your eternal servants,

*Your devotees in Sao Paulo, Brazil,*

## Seattle

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Twenty-one years ago you came here to Seattle. You preached enthusiastically and installed Lord Jagannātha, the second set of Jagannātha deities installed in ISKCON. Here are some excerpts of what you said about Seattle at the time:

"Today there was a television station interview in my apartment. They came with big cameras and microphones, and I played harmonium and one lady interviewed with a few questions. They took also pictures of my altar with Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa Deities, and Lord Jagannātha and the cover pictures of my *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Teachings of Lord Caitanya. So* it will be on the newscast at 5:00 this evening, in color, and we will go and see how they have done it. They also came to our class last night and took pictures of our students dancing in ecstasy. So this should help our movement here in Seattle, as so many people will see and come to our temple. " (Oct. 3, 1968, to Yamunā)

"I have come here to Seattle and on Saturday last, the 21st instant. And the report is that in this place, our preaching of Kṛṣṇa consciousness has great potency. So I shall begin my classes today. And they have arranged some lectures also. Let me see what will happen." (Sept 23, 1968, to Yamunā)

"I think Seattle branch will come out very successful, because we have drawn the attention of the students of the university here." (Oct 6, 1968, to Brahmānanda)

"Here in Seattle, things are going very nicely. Last night, there was one radio program for two hours, and the *kīrtana* party sang so many songs and prayers very nicely." (Oct 10, 1968, to Śivānanda)

There are many similar letters. Now, in 1989, we are still working to make Seattle Kṛṣṇa conscious. With a straw in our teeth, we beg you to bless us with your mercy so that we may follow in your glorious footsteps.

*Your servants at ISKCON Seattle,*

## Soviet Union

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

There is one picture of you standing on the Lenin Hills with your elbows leaning on the wall as you look on the view of Moscow before you. You face is grave and thoughtful, and below you, at your feet, is a big city with factories and plants, roaring cars and trucks, and crowds of people seeking sense gratification.

What did you see at that moment, looking out on the demoniac city spread before you, a city where there was room for everything except God consciousness? The miseries of people bewildered by their blind leaders? Millions of cheaters and the cheated, wasting their valuable human lives? But the vision of a spiritual master can penetrate much further than an ordinary person's, for he sees past, present, and future. Who could imagine during that fearful time, which is now officially called "the period of stagnation," that hundreds of effulgent devotees in *dhotīs* and *kūrtas,* with *mṛdaṅgas* and *karatalas,* would come out onto the streets of Moscow chanting the holy names? But you saw that, we are sure. You saw thousands of your books miraculously appear in Moscow, and from there spread all over the Soviet Union. You saw people who were frustrated and tired from fruitless materialism snatch your books out of each other's hands, feeling that these books were their last chance. You saw far away from Moscow, in Armenia, buildings come down on the heads of people who had forgotten how they were supposed to live, and you saw your devotees from all over the country rush there to distribute *prasādam* and the holy name to the victims of the earthquake. And not only that: you looked on as fifty-nine devotees from different cities gathered in the Sheremetyevo airport to get onto the plane that would carry them to Calcutta. You saw these Russians, Lithuanians, Latvians, Armenians, etc., walk on the holy land of Bhārata-bhūmi and then go back, bringing with them, to the heart of one of the most demoniac countries on earth. Deities of Lord Jagannātha. You saw also that which we, with our mundane vision, cannot see yet: You saw the inauguration of the marble temple of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Moscowīśvara and the crowds of people eager to take *darśana* of the Deities of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa and Gaura-Nitāi. You saw that, instead of tanks and cannons, the chariots of Lord Jagannātha were being pulled along in Red Square, and that *cakras* were decorating the domes of the Kremlin cathedrals.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are sure that on the day you gazed out over Moscow you saw much more than this, all of which will manifest in due course of time. But on this auspicious day of Vyāsa-pūjā, we pray to you: Please give us faith and determination so that we, helpless and insignificant as we are, can become obedient instruments in your hands, so that we, like the famous spider, can bring our grains of sand and put them in that glorious building you are constructing, so that we can see at least a part of that vision which was open to your sight.

Every day new people join your glorious mission, realizing that in this dark age of Kali there is no other way, there is no other way, there is no other way. It is impossible to list the names of all your followers here in the USSR, so while begging for your mercy, we sign this offering simply

*Your fallen servants in the Soviet Union.*

## St. Louis

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet. All glories to your ISKCON movement and to all the devotees who are taking part in carrying the responsibility of maintaining and pushing forward your instructions.

In the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (Fourth Canto, Chapter 9, Text 11, purport) you state, "The significant point in Dhruva Mahārāja's statement is that he wanted the association of pure devotees. Transcendental devotional service cannot be complete and cannot be relishable without the association of devotees. We have therefore established the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. Anyone who is trying to be aloof from this Kṛṣṇa consciousness Society and yet engage in Kṛṣṇa consciousness is living in a great hallucination, for this is not possible." How can so many disciples live outside ISKCON?

From the same purport: "From this statement by Dhruva Mahārāja it is clear that unless one is associated with devotees, his devotional service does not mature; it does not become distinct from material activities." So many devotees are frustrated—disappointed expectations! But where do you say to leave? So why would devotees leave your ISKCON?

"Indeed, devotees sometimes compare the color of gold to that of bright golden stool. Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu has instructed devotees not to be allured by golden ornaments and beautifully decorated women. *Na dhanaṁ na janaṁ na sundarīm:* a devotee should not be allured by gold, beautiful women or the prestige of having many followers." (*Bhāg.* 5.16.21) And again in the *Bhāgavatam* (5.14.35, purport), "The monkeylike conditioned soul first becomes attached to sex, and when intercourse actually takes place he becomes more attached. He then acquires some material comforts—apartment, house, food, friends, wealth, and so on. In order to acquire these things he has to cheat others, and this creates enmity even among the most intimate friends. Sometimes this enmity is created between the conditioned soul and the father or spiritual master. Unless one is firmly fixed in the regulative principles, one may perform mischievous acts, even if one is a member of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. We therefore advise our disciples to strictly follow the regulative principles; otherwise the most important movement for the upliftment of humanity will be hampered due to dissension among its members. Those who are serious about pushing forward this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement should remember this and strictly follow the regulative principles so that their minds will not be disturbed."

Our fervent prayer, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is to be able to assimilate these instructions and try to help others to do so also. We pray that we may be instrumental in bringing your ISKCON family closer together. We pray that we may never give up trying in this endeavor. We especially pray to stay engaged in *saṅkīrtana,* for that is our real saving grace. Not only will this save ourselves and this movement, but the conditioned souls as well. So this is the most realistic business endeavor. And as you have stated, this book distribution is so important that all other programs are secondary!

*Your servants in St. Louis,*

Govinda Datta dāsa / Cakrī dāsa / Dayānanda dāsa / Garga Muni dāsa / Gaura-Nitāi dāsa / Gopīśvara dāsa / Bhakta Winston / Śāntātmā-devī dāsī / Tulasī-devī dāsī / Kṛṣṇa-Balarāma-devī dāsī

## Stockholm

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

After I had barely survived my birth, they told me in school that I am free and I should do what I think. So I tried to stand for my opinion; surrounded by similar speculators, I tried to seek happiness in so-called freedom of the mind. Drowning in an ocean of dualities, miserable and bewildered, blindly trying to follow all kinds of relative concepts, ending up exploited by my senses, I finally met your disciples, radiant and determined to please you. Every word they uttered was filled with love for you. They told me, "Śrīla Prabhupāda is the absolute authority because he is serving the Absolute One!" And then I saw you, suddenly and unexpectedly, in the airport hall, arriving to save me from confusion by delivering the Absolute Truth.

Suddenly everything was so easy. Suddenly there was your absolute shelter, cooling the heat of mental speculation. In my initiation ceremony I forgot one of the regulative principles; you just smiled mildly and said, "Just chant Hare Kṛṣṇa" and gave me the *japa* beads. I was still bewildered, bull knew, "Here is absolute shelter; now the suffering will be soon over."

How simple and how sublime your words: "Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Lord, and we are His eternal servants."

Such simple service you gave me: to distribute your books, take your *prasādam,* and be happy.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give me the strength to be simple and to seek shelter more intensely in your absolute words.

This Kali-yuga is a horrible time. The preachers of relativity are slaughtering the bewildered souls, who are in turn seeking shelter in the desert of voidness. Let me cling more tightly to your authority and not let the speculations cover the Absolute Truth. The conditioned souls are thirsty for this absolute authority, but the demons leave them with nothing but their democracies and parliaments, ruled indeed by the strict dictatorship of sense gratification. The ignorant people do not know that there is nothing beyond the Absolute Truth, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead. Only when this absolute understanding is firmly established in their hearts will real peace be possible.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda! Please let our words become potent so that we may convince these frightened souls that there is no loss in surrendering to the shelter of your lotus feet. Everything is possible by achieving that firm faith in your absolute instructions. Please protect us from deviation. *Māyā* can make black seem white and white seem black. But wherever your books are, there the reality becomes evident, eliminating the darkness of illusion.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us absolute shelter in the form of your absolute instructions. Now it is up to us to worship them by becoming obedient.

All glories to Your Divine Grace.

(written by Maṇidhara dāsa)

*Your servants at the Hare Kṛṣṇa Center in Stockholm, Sweden,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Switzerland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Our lives belong to your book distribution mission. By the distribution of your books, our lives becomes more and more successful.

You came down to this world, sent by Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu, just to write these wonderful books. You are the *senāpati-bhakta* prophesied in the scriptures, the commander in chief for all devotees to spread Lord Caitanya's teachings in the twentieth century, and indeed for the next ten thousand years, all over the world, and from here all over the universe. Therefore you are known as Prabhupāda, the spiritual master at whose lotus feet all *prabhus* take shelter.

Your purports are your transcendental ecstasies. Therefore everybody coming in touch with your books becomes purified and can attain eternal love for Kṛṣṇa. Only when we get a taste for reading your books and distributing them, either one by one or a by the full set, will our lives be successful. These books are nondifferent from you; therefore we are always with you when we read and distribute them. For the next ten thousand years your books are the lawbooks for the whole human society. Therefore we have to become like lawyers, knowing everything within your books. Your last instruction was: "There is nothing new to say; everything is in my books. Therefore if you want to know me, read my books."

The BBT is your heart, and *lakṣmī is* your blood. Therefore it is our duty to serve your BBT. You are the BBT's founder-*ācārya*, and when we sell your books according to the mercy principle of "double the BBT price," the people can appreciate your books more. We actually can realize that when we follow this principle, you are always with us. Although in this world there are always problems, when we distribute your books the problems fly away.

We want to make your desire more and more our desire by giving everyone in this country at least one book and every gentleman a full set. We know that you become very happy when we give these books to everyone, without discrimination. You gave us our *prabhu-datta-deśa,* the country where we can serve Your Divine Grace by trying to go everywhere, in every village and city, Hans to Hans, door to door, shop to shop, office to office, shopping center to shopping center, and airport to airport—wherever people are. We never come to the end of book distribution; by your grace we always find more people who want to buy your books, or the same people who buy more books. By your divine mercy, may it never stop. Please give us more and more the ability, strength, and intelligence to expand this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement in the form of your books. Please send more sincere devotees who will help to distribute this great mercy. In this way the whole world will become overflooded by your transcendental books. Then everybody will drown in this unlimited mercy ocean.

By your mercy we can always taste the sweet competition between the BBT production managers, who always make arrangements to fill up the warehouse with your books, and the book distributors. The distributors cannot tolerate seeing that the warehouse is full, and therefore they want to empty it. But somehow, although we try very hard to empty it, it always increases miraculously. By your mercy and the mercy of the BBT trustee and his servants, we always have all the books we need. Thank you very much.

We know that we never can pay back the debt we owe you for what you have given us. But sometimes we are like little children, who don't know the value of gold. We try somehow to pay you back, although it is impossible and foolish, by surrendering fully to your book distribution mission. By engaging our body, mind, intelligence, words, and freedom in your service, by helping you to fulfill the order of your spiritual master, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Goswami Mahārāja Ṭhākura, we are trying to pay back a tiny fraction of our immense debt to you. We are like puppets, and you are the puppet master. The puppet has no freedom or independence from the master's will. Therefore let us dance according to your plan. Otherwise, there is no happiness in this world.

There is no happiness in this world other than in following your instructions and taking shelter at your lotus feet. Your lotus feet are the only shelter in the three worlds. And we want to distribute your books so that the whole world will know about the glories of the pure devotee of the Lord. Therefore how can we peacefully sit back and give up book distribution as long as we haven't accomplished this goal? We are helpless little children, and Your Divine Grace has left us a big, big heritage. We pray again and again to your divine lotus feet that we all become more and more absorbed in your preaching mission.

Jaya Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakācārya Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad Abhaya Caraṇāravinda Bhaktivedanta Swami Mahārāja Prabhupāda-kī Jaya! Śrīla Prabhupāda's transcendental book distribution kī Jaya!

*Your servants at the Swiss Yātrā.*

## Sydney

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most auspicious day. Once again we have been blessed to have the opportunity to meditate on your divine appearance, the repercussions of which are still touching the lives of millions of *jīvas.*

All over the world, from China to the Amazonian jungles, the chanting of "Hare Kṛṣṇa" and "Jaya Prabhupāda!" is resounding, giving Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this most auspicious day. Once again we have been blessed to have the opportunity to meditate on your divine appearance, the repercussions of which are still touching the lives of millions of *jīvas.*

All over the world, from China to the Amazonian jungles, the chanting of "Hare Kṛṣṇa" and "Jaya Prabhupāda!" is resounding, giving hope and joy to an otherwise hopeless and despondent world. And your influence is increasing daily, unlike that of materially motivated personalities who may accumulate followers and power but are ultimately discredited and have their influence vanquished by the passage of time. Your association has created a purely spiritual society that will continue to expand and flourish as long as we, its members, remain connected with you by rigidly following your instructions and selflessly dedicating our lives to fulfilling your desires. And what could be sweeter or more sublime than discarding all our material conceptions of ourselves, along with all of the anxieties and sufferings that go along with them, and immersing ourselves in your divine order to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness?

So today we pray to you: Please enable us to be fully engaged in your service, please give us the ability to maintain the structure that you have given us, and please bestow upon us the pure enthusiasm to increase, especially the preaching spirit of book distribution, *hari-nāma saṅkīrtana,* and *prasādam* distribution, which you so perfectly displayed and so meticulously passed on to us.

By using this Vyāsa-pūjā day to meditate on the divine purpose of your appearance and the immense nature of your compassionate preaching activities, we can get a great impetus to either dedicate ourselves for the first time or re-dedicate ourselves to your preaching mission, thus freeing ourselves and others from the clutches of the material energy.

*Your servants of ISKCON Sydney, Australia,*

## Taiwan

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

For those of us who have accepted you as our spiritual master and grand-spiritual master, it is again the time to meditate deeply upon the glories of Your Divine Grace and try to offer some prayers on this auspicious day. Fallen as we are, being about as far west as we possible could be compared to Gaudadesa, it is only appropriate to repeat the *pranama-mantra* that you have taught us for your glorification.

Amidst all the vices of Kali-yuga, we derive strength, enthusiasm, introspection, and purification simply by your benevolent countenance and your divine instructions. In order to present Lord Caitanya's philosophy nicely in a deeply-rooted non-Vedic culture, we try for worldly sophistication, and sometimes we are enticed by Maya, but the teachings in your books protect us. Just to rationalize our material desires, we put forth our foolish arguments and sometimes in our audacity mistreat others, yet the spiritual program and your representatives—the senior devotees—guide us.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are but insignificant parts of a complete instrument, your spiritual organization in Lord Caitanya's *saṅkīrtana* movement. To serve your instructions, your ISKCON, and your BBT—these are our aspirations for perfection.

*Your servants at the Taiwan yātrā,*

Yaśomatī-suta dāsa / Sanātana Gosvāmī dāsa / Ādiśekha dāsa / Sevānanda dāsa / Bhakta Jim / Harināma-devī dāsī / Śravaṇīyā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Kitty

## Toronto

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this auspicious day of your appearance in this world, we at your temple in Toronto, Canada, would like you to know that we are enlivened and enthusiastic in your service.

Your programs of Deity worship, book distribution, restaurant, theater, *harināma,* etc., are all in place and functioning.

Gone is the chaos and confusion of past years; gone is the bitterness and frustration in the ranks of the devotees. Back is the ebb and flow of nice, natural relationships between Vaiṣṇavas inside and outside your temple and with our large community of Eastern and Western devotees.

Back is the respect for our GBC and temple president in a mood of love and trust. Back is the feeling of "growing mood" that you established, Śrīla Prabhupāda, a mood in which everyone feels part of the plan-making and the responsibility to push on with the transcendental activity of distributing "this burden of love" that you requested us to do.

Plans are underway for an exterior face-lift for the walls and roof, and for interior redecorating of your temple here. ISKCON Toronto has one of the most prestigious buildings in North America, thanks to Your Divine Grace. It is strategically located; indeed, its location is referred to as "the gateway to Toronto." Toronto is booming and is making itself felt as an international city, and our temple is preparing itself to take advantage of this. All of this you saw when you instructed your disciples to get this building.

To sum up, the overall feeling in your ISKCON, Śrīla Prabhupāda, if Toronto is any indication, is one of optimism and spiritual growth, a sweetening of the fruit of the Caitanya tree.

*From all your servants at New Remuṇā-dhāma (Toronto, Canada).*

## Trinidad

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this most auspicious day of your appearance in this world, Śrī Vyāsa-pūjā, we beg you to please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet, which alone can deliver us from this miserable material existence. Without such mercy—the dust of the lotus feet of such a venerable Vaiṣṇava as yourself—we are simply drowning in this ocean of material existence. Your lotus feet are the actual boat affording us safe passage over this dangerous ocean. Because you appeared as the direct representative of Lord Kṛṣṇa in disciplic succession coming from Him, you can easily rescue us from our most unfortunate, pitiful condition. Only one who is very compassionate and is actually empowered by the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, can deliver the fallen souls of this would. Let us pray, then, that we can be sincere, humble, and intelligent enough to always seek the shelter of your lotus feet by submissively hearing, understanding, and carrying out your divine instructions. For only when we become very serious about serving your lotus feet will we receive safe passage out of this temporary, miserable world. Moreover, we will also be able to bestow your mercy on so many countless conditioned souls who are simply rotting in this abominable condition of material existence for want of your mercy.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for allowing us to serve you and for accepting our insignificant service. We beg that you please keep us under the soothing shelter of your fearless lotus feet.

*Your servants at ISKCON Longdenville, Trinidad,*

## Udhampur

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Our dearmost, revered Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most fallen obeisances at your lotuslike feet, which are so purifying that whosoever comes in contact with them, even by chance, gets the benediction of *kṛṣṇa-prema* and becomes blessed. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who saved millions and millions from the clutches of dreadful *māyā* (the illusory energy of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa), and gave them the purifying process of devotional service. Your divine self gave the whole world real meaning to life.

Nearly eighteen months ago your movement entered this city of springs and hills under the gracious leadership of one of your movement's stalwart *sannyāsīs* and spiritual sons, Nava-yogendra Swami Mahārāja. Since then your mercy has created amazing miracles in the city and its surroundings. We started a street chanting program sixteen months ago, going out every morning at 5:00 daily, and has been continuing nonstop since then. Hundreds of devotees take part in it daily. We all are indebted to you, birth after birth, for making our lives sublime with this process of *harināma-saṅkīrtana.*

We are at a loss while penning these few words as an offering at your lotus feet, because we, being fallen, conditioned souls, are incapable of glorifying a pure devotee like yourself.

We beg for your mercy and blessings so that we shall always have the association of your pure devotees and thereby be saved from falling down to a hellish condition of life.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda! We fall down at your lotus like feet as your unworthy servants.
