## New Jaipur

**Spiritual Titans**

In studying the lives of Śrīla Prabhupāda, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, and Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, we can see striking parallels in their unparalleled activities. These mahā-bhāgavatas exhibit the superexcellent qualities of pure devotees so brilliantly that even gross materialists are captivated, impressed, even stunned, amazed, and transformed by seeing their behavior. There is no mistaking their position as self-realized, self-effulgent, empowered *mahātmās* who came for the deliverance of the world.

These three *mahā-bhāgavata* devotees showed extraordinary compassion for the bewildered denizens of the universe and acted without a pinch of personal consideration. They endeavored to deliver the suffering world with all the facilities at their command: mind, body, words, followers, money—with everything that came to them by the Lord's unique arrangements.

They wrote volumes of books, all brilliantly and perfectly composed, designed to penetrate and transform the hearts of their readers. Their taste for being always immersed in the vibration and audition of the Lord's name and glories was deep, unfathomable. They utilized every moment of every day in broadcasting the name, form, qualities, pastimes, and philosophy of the Supreme Lord.

Although they performed superhuman, liberated, and empowered work, they never thought that they had any good qualifications. They saw that their predecessors were actually great. Like Hanumān, they accomplished impossible missions and more than fulfilled the ardent desires of the Lord, yet they always felt that they were unable to do much for Him.

They could see the future, and they invoked the Lord's mercy with their earnest prayers for the deliverance of the world. The year 1896 was momentous, for it was both the year of Śrīla Prabhupāda's birth and the year that Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu's teachings reached the Western world in the form of Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura's book *Shri Chaitanya Mahāprabhu: His Life and Precepts.* This was no accident. The Ṭhākura had earnestly prayed for the liberation of the world in the mood of Śrī Advaita invoking the descent of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. He had written in the *Sajjana-toṣaṇī:* "Śrīman Mahāprabhu did not descend with His associates to deliver a certain number of human beings in the land of India, but rather His purpose was to deliver and uplift all living beings in all countries of the world by practicing the eternal religion of all souls." He quoted Caitanya Mahāprabhu from the *Caitanya-bhāgavata:* "My name will be preached in all the countries and towns that exist throughout the world." (Cb. *Antya* 4.126)

The Ṭhākura then prayerfully invoked the Lord and expressed his heartfelt desire: "There are many kinds of religion in the world, and among all of them the highest development of religion is the congregational chanting of the Supreme Lord's divine names. Of this there is no doubt. Alas! When will that day come when all greatly fortunate souls in countries such as England, France, Russia, Prussia, and America will take up banners, kettledrums, mṛdaṅgas, and karatālas and thus cause the ecstatic waves of harināma-kīrtana and the singing of "Jaya śacīnandana, jaya śacīnandana-ki jaya!" to rise in the streets of their towns and cities? O when will that day come when pure and transcendental Vaiṣṇava prema will be the only religion of all souls and all tiny sectarian religions will meet in the unlimited and universal religion of Vaiṣṇavism, as rivers merge into the great ocean? O when will that day come?"

And then, amazingly, he penned another prediction which can be equally applied to not only to Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura but also, most certainly, to Śrīla Prabhupāda: "A personality will soon appear to preach the teachings of Lord Caitanya and move unrestrictedly over the whole world with His message." Of course, Śrīla Prabhupāda credited Śrīla Sarasvatī Ṭhākura with this by writing, "In the same order as Kardama Muni, about one hundred years ago Ṭhākura Bhaktivinoda also wanted to beget a child who could preach the philosophy and teachings of Lord Caitanya to the fullest extent. By his prayers to the Lord he had as his child Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Mahārāja, who at the present moment is preaching the philosophy of Lord Caitanya throughout the entire world through his bona fide disciples." On the other hand, the personality who actually repeatedly circled the world, who moved "unrestrictedly over the whole world," was undoubtedly Śrīla Prabhupāda. So the prediction applies equally to Śrīla Prabhupāda and Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura, who accompanied Śrīla Prabhupāda to the West and from whom Śrīla Prabhupāda was never separated for a moment. And most certainly that great figure, Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, whose prayers brought such a wealth of association to the impoverished world in the form of his son and in the form of his son's foremost disciple, also accompanied Śrīla Prabhupāda. After all, Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura had written:

He reasons ill who tells that Vaiṣṇavas die,  
When thou art living still in sound!  
The Vaiṣṇavas die to live, and living try  
To spread a holy life around!

And Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura further clarifies this point in the *Bhāgavata* when he writes profoundly, regarding the great commentators of Vedic literature, "These great souls were not mere luminaries like comets appearing in the firmament for a while and disappearing as soon as their mission is done. They are like so many suns shining all along to give light and heat to the succeeding generations. Long time yet to roll on when they will be succeeded by others of their sublime mind. beauty, and calibre."

Just as Advaita Ācārya had invoked the descent of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu for the salvation of the souls of the damned, so also along with Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu came the descent of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa, the Kali-yuga *avatāra* who is forever prepared to shed His mercy. So also the great eternal associates of the Lord have descended to keep the mercy of the great current of chanting flowing strongly. The earnest entreaties of those great souls, who invoke the mercy of the Lord with every breath, bring inconceivable mercy to those lost in the darkness of material existence. Lord Caitanya told Śrīla Vāsudeva Datta Ṭhākura when he offered to accept all the sins of all the souls in the entire universe, if the Lord would deliver them: "Whatever a pure devotee wants from his master, Lord Kṛṣṇa doubtlessly grants because He has no duty other than to fulfill the desire of His devotee.... Because of your honest desire, all living entities within the universe will be delivered, for Kṛṣṇa does not have to do anything to deliver all the living entities of the universe."

By the prayers of Śrīla Saccidānanda Bhaktivinoda Ṭhākura, the great personifications of compassion and mercy, Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Ṭhākura and Śrīla A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, came to fulfill his desires. Śrīla Prabhupāda also saw the future of Kali-yuga and knew of Lord Caitanya's Golden Age. He prayed for and was granted a special benediction by Lord Caitanya for the benefit of us all. He was granted that his *śikṣā* would have such liberating power that it would continue to act for 10,000 years. Śrīla Prabhupāda certified it. This was not an idle boast. This was a statement of fact from one who was the very emblem of humility. His *śikṣā* is sufficiently powerful, by the grace of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, to deliver conditioned souls throughout the Golden Age of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu!!

By the grace of the nondifferent pure desires of these three *mahā-bhāgavatas* and all pure followers of the Lord, the universe will be delivered. Billions of souls will go back to Godhead by the mercy of the pure Vaiṣṇavas. Thus, we see the power of one who is as good as God.

*Your servants at New Jaipur (Washington, Mississippi),*

Nityānanda dāsa / Rūpa-vilāsa dāsa / Karṇāmṛta dāsa / Ṛṣi Kumara dāsa / Vimala dāsa / Puṇḍarīka dāsa / Candrikā-devī dāsī / Kanyā-kumārī-devī dāsī / Kumārī-kāntā-devī dāsī / Saucārgā-devī dāsī / Śarad-vihāri-devī dāsī / Ramaṇīyā-devī dāsī / and all the children

## Nova Morada

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your holy lotus feet, which are, without any doubt, the only secure shelter in these times of confusions and doubts.

Certainly, Śrīla Prabhupāda, to offer flowers, prayers, and poetry to the *guru* is an aspect of a sincere follower and disciple's etiquette. But the best offering we can give you is to render our insignificant and useless lives so you can utilize them as you wish.

This very same mood that we have on this auspicious day of your appearance, we want to apply every day until the end of our existence in this material body.

We want to be able to always remember the expert and unexpected way that you appear in our lives—the way that you awaken us to the real life, which is *kṛṣṇa-kathā* and *kṛṣṇa-sevā.*

We want to be able to always remember the way you taught us about chanting, dancing, playing the instruments, cooking, preaching, etc.

In few words, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you gave real meaning to our useless and hopeless lives.

For this reason and many others the only thing we want and expect is to serve your lotus feet eternally. Because you are not physically present, the only way we find to serve you is to serve in the ISKCON movement, by faithfully following your instructions, especially chanting sixteen rounds every day, follow the four regulative principles, living in the association of the devotees, and preaching to the innocent.

Please, *gurudeva,* remember our fallen existence and bless us so we can become more useful and qualified instruments of yours.

*From your disciples and granddisciples in Nova Morada, Portugal,*

Puruṣatrāya Swami / Kuladeva dāsa / Anantadeva dāsa / Locana dāsa / Bhakta Cide / Bhakta Min / Bhakta Vases / Lakṣmaṇā-devī dāsī / Padmākṣī and Jāhnavī / Bhaktine Angela / Lakṣmī-Nṛsiṁha and Narottama

## Pada-yātrā

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dearest Śrīla Prabhupāda,

When you arrived in the USA in 1965, you looked upon the "bleak and dirty skyline" of such a godless civilization and realized the difficulty of your sacred mission. You wrote, "O Lord, I am just like a puppet in Your hands. So if You have brought me here to dance, then make me dance, make me dance, O Lord, make me dance as You like. I have no devotion, nor do I have any knowledge, but I have strong faith in the Holy Name of Krishna. I have been designated as "Bhaktivedanta," and now if you like you can fulfill the real purport of Bhaktivedanta..."

Similarly, we on *pada-yātrā* look upon this long, bleak, dirty road of a civilization that was once the most God conscious, now become entrapped into *māyā’s* mission. O Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are just like a puppet show in your hands. So if you have brought us here to walk and chant, then make us walk and chant, make us walk and chant, O Śrīla Prabhupāda, make us walk and chant at least until your First Centennial Appearance Day. We have no devotion, nor do we have any knowledge, but we have strong faith in your mission. We have been designated as *“pada-yātrīs,”* and now, if you like, you can fulfill the real purport of *pada-yātrā.*

So many of us *pada-yātrīs* want to "take a break" from this glorious program because of the difficulties that arise from trying to spread your glories and the holy name around in each village, town, and city. But we do not realize the extreme difficulties that you single-handedly took upon yourself to deliver the conditioned souls around the world. We see and feel this country known as India with each step and recollect the past glories of Bhāratavarśa, but at the same time we see all the Kali-yuga symptoms slowly swallowing up the last of the truly pious souls. So instead of thinking we want a break, we should meditate upon your unlimitedly determined efforts to spread the Holy Name, which in turn will give us more inspiring strength.

We are constantly praying for your causeless mercy in order to continue on with this walking and chanting program. We hope that by some of our preaching efforts you are pleased with us, and if you are not, then please forgive us for our offenses at your lotus feet.

*Your insignificant walking servants,*

Lokanātha Swami / Jaya Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Śyāmakuṇḍa dāsa / Śrī Jayadeva dāsa / Paraśurāma dāsa / Śrīmān Paṇḍita dāsa / Oṁ-tat-sat dāsa / Hari-caraṇa dāsa / Upendra Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Guhya dāsa / Śvetadvīpa dāsa / Viṣṇudūta dāsa / Caitanyacandra dāsa / Bhakta Ganesh / Bhim Bhai / Bhakta Martin / Shyamasundar / Nimiohi / Rādhākuṇḍa-devī dāsī / Gaurī-devī dāsī / Sītārāṇī-devī dāsī / Praśānta-devī dāsī / Navayauvana-devī dāsī / Hira Ben / Lakṣmī the elephant, our four devoted bulls, and Jaya Vijaya dāsa

## Prabhupāda Saṅkīrtana Center

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept my obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Their Lordships Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, our most worshipable Deities.

You are very kind to have come to the Western world and delivered us from the darkness of ignorance and engaged us so nicely in the transcendental loving service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda.

Although you were living very comfortably in the holy *dhāma,* Vṛndāvana, where Lord Kṛṣṇa is eternally performing His glorious pastimes, on the order of your spiritual master. His Divine Grace Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Gosvāmī Prabhupāda, you departed from India for the Western world on August 13, 1965.

Your confidence in your spiritual master and Kṛṣṇa enabled you to overcome seasickness and two heart attacks. You were duty-bound and determined to bring the message of Kṛṣṇa to us souls who were suffering so severely in Maya's kingdom.

You overwhelmed us with your love. As Dr. Misra said, "You are the incarnation of love."

Out of love for you we shaved our heads, decorated our foreheads with *tilaka,* and dressed ourselves in *dhotīs.* We took complete shelter of you, disregarding everything we had ever heard or learned before. We carried out your order and went to the streets and chanted the holy names of the Lord—Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rāma, Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma, Hare Hare—distributed delicious *luglus* and sold *Back to Godhead* magazines for fifty cents a copy, and we were happy, very happy.

We ate lots of *capātīs,* rice, *dāl,* and *sabjī,* along with sweet rice, coconut chutney, *laḍḍus, samosās,* and eggplant *pakorās—*all your mercy.

We believed everything you told us: that there is God and He is Kṛṣṇa, the all-attractive, and that His representative, the spiritual master, descends to deliver the fallen souls on the Lord's behalf. We naturally accepted you as Kṛṣṇa's representative, our *guru.* We didn't have to look for the bona fide spiritual master: you delivered yourself to us. So without anxiety we engaged in Kṛṣṇa's loving service.

We told everyone we met how wonderful you are and how wonderful your Kṛṣṇa is, and they also became your loving disciples. And we saw your movement grow rapidly, and it is still growing rapidly because more and more, all over the world, individuals are directly taking shelter of you, and you are so kindly accepting their service.

It is said in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* that "by regularly hearing the *Bhāgavatam* and rendering service unto the pure devotee, all that is troublesome to the heart is practically destroyed, and loving service unto the glorious Lord, who is praised with transcendental songs, is established as an irrevocable fact."

Because the hearts of your new followers are becoming soft, we know that you are accepting their service. Now they are going into the streets and telling everyone about how wonderful you are and how wonderful Kṛṣṇa is.

And now with the mercy of the Vaiṣṇavas ever increasing, the poor, wretched souls of Kali-yuga are gaining hope that they can be saved.

Under your unifying banner, Śrīla Prabhupāda, accepting you as their *guru,* the devotees are enthusiastically dancing and chanting "Hare Kṛṣṇa!" and "Jaya Prabhupāda!" in the streets again, with tears in their eyes. And everyone is becoming joyful.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, eternal father, protector, savior, and spiritual master, you live forever, and your followers live with you. That's the fact.

Please continue to lead us on to victory over the vices of this age. Help us to follow strictly your morning and evening *sādhana* program. Help us execute your order of chanting loudly "Hare Kṛṣṇa!" in the streets, distributing *prasādam* profusely, and selling unlimited amounts of your wonderful books of Vedic knowledge to the fallen souls in this rapidly degrading age of Kali. We need you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We cannot live a moment without you. Your mercy is what we are made of. Please give us the shelter of your lotus feet, where all your disciples, present and future, find real peace and security.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, our ever well-wisher.

*Your servants at the Prabhupāda Saṅkīrtana Center, New York City,*

Kapīndra dāsa / Caitanya-priya dāsa / Kauśalyā-devī dāsī / Bhakta Rashid / Bhakta Nisa

## Salzburg

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**I offer my respectful obeisances to His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who somehow or other managed to save this wretched fool from the eternal dungeon of materialistic life. He is the pure Vaiṣṇava** ***ācārya*** **who delivers everyone in the Western world from the plague of impersonlism and voidism.**

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda, who selflessly—at the risk of his life—crossed the ocean from India to America and landed like the king of swans in Kali-yuga New York City, the capital of the crows.

Let my mind bathe in the nectarean remembrance of Śrīla Prabhupāda, who sat on a *chādar* on the bare floor of a Bowery flop-house in the late sixties, and who sat on magnificent gold-plate and solid-silver *vyāsāsanas* in over one hundred palatial temples in the seventies.

Let me worship his teachings, his translations and purports of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Bhagavad-gītā As It Is, Śrī Caitanya-caritāmṛta, Nectar of Devotion, Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Nectar of Instruction, Śrī Īśopaniṣad, Easy Journey to Other Planets,* etc., etc. These transcendental literatures have overthrown all demoniac influences in this age. They are full of spiritual potencies, being nondifferent from Kṛṣṇa. They appeared due to the pure and selfless devotion of their servant—His Divine Grace A. C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda.

My obeisances again and again to the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda, the founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness. The effulgence of his lotus feet purifies the three worlds, including the six continents of this planet. His purity and charming devotion, his sincerity of purpose and scholarly erudition, his unflinching determination and majestic composure—all of these have won the hearts of thousands of men and women who became his initiated disciples. He led them through an odyssey of ten years, establishing the banner of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu and His *saṅkīrtana* movement in America, Europe, Latin America, Russia, India, Australia, Africa—in every town and village! Like an expert *mahā-ratha* from bygone ages, like Arjuna, he fought the dwarfs of the Kali-yuga, conquering the earth on behalf of Kṛṣṇa.

Though I may be fallen, most degraded and foolish, let me try to humbly serve his *vapuḥ* and *vāṇī.*

*The devotees of the Bhaktivedanta Institute in Salzburg, Austria,*

Jaya-gurudeva dāsa Adhikārī / Jatāyu dāsa Adhikārī / Jimuta dāsa Adhikārī / Bhakta Peter / Bhakta Herbert / Vṛndāvana-devī dāsī / Latā-devī dāsī / Ṣaḍ-bhuja-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Angelica

**Children**

Śeṣa dāsa / Nanda dāsa / Nimāi dāsa / Rāma dāsa / Govinda / Jaya Nitāi

## Save-the-Cow

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

"For a Sanātanist (a follower of Vedic principles) it is the duty of every householder to have cows and bulls as household paraphernalia, not only for drinking milk, but also for deriving religious principles. The Sanātanist worships cows on religious principles and respects *brāhmaṇas.* The cow's milk is required for the sacrificial fire, and by performing sacrifices, the householder can be happy. The cow's calf not only is beautiful to look at, but also gives satisfaction to the cow, and so she delivers as much milk as possible. But in Kali-yuga, the calves are separated from the cows as early as possible for purposes which may not be mentioned in these pages of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.* The cow stands with tears in her eyes, the *śūdra* milkman draws milk from the cow artificially, and when there is no milk the cow is sent to be slaughtered. These greatly sinful acts are responsible for all the troubles in present society. People do not know what they are doing in the name of economic development. The influence of Kali will keep them in the darkness of ignorance. Despite all endeavors for peace and prosperity, they must try to see the cows and bulls happy in all respects. Foolish people do not know how one earns happiness by making the cows and bulls happy, but it is a fact by the laws of nature. Let us take it from the authority of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and adopt the principles for the total happiness of humanity." *(Bhāg.* 1.17.3purport)

"The human society should recognize the importance of the cow and the bull and thus give all protection to these important animals, following in the footsteps of Mahārāja Parīkṣit. For protecting the cows and brahminical culture, the Lord, who is very kind to the cow and the *brāhmaṇas (go brāhmaṇa-hitāya ca),* will be pleased with us and will bestow upon us real peace." *(Bhāg.* 1.17.9purport)

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have taught us that by properly using the cow and bull, human society will be protected, prosperous, and happy. We at New Rāmaṇa-reti were inspired by our Godbrothers and Godsisters of Gītā-nāgarī’s Adopt-a-Cow to program to try to spread your teachings of cow protection in the southeastern United States, and thus Save-the-Cow was developed.

We are humbly trying to develop a congregational community of donors for and participants in our program of cow and ox protection and preaching. We show cow and ox use by practical example on the farm. We send out newsletters with quotations from your books and instructions on how to try to apply your teachings practically, and we give out *prasādam* milk sweets to the members regularly. Our ox teams go to local fairs and create quite a sensation, which leads to discussion of vegetarianism, Vedic culture, and other aspects of Kṛṣṇa conscious philosophy. (We have learned that many people don't even know that an ox is a male "cow"!) Our membership is gradually growing, and the local Indian community is especially favorable.

We beg your blessings that this program may become more and more ecstatic and successful, according to your desire and the desire of the Supreme Lord Govinda, pleaser of the cows.

*Your servants at Save-the-Cow (Alachua, Florida),*

Brajendranandana dāsa / Tamrapaṇī dāsa / Keśava dāsa / Īśvara Purī dāsa / and Kamrā-devī dāsī

## Singapore

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Only by the grace and blessings of the pure devotees of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa can the conditioned souls get out of the struggle for material existence and attain devotional service at the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please bestow your blessings and guide us so that we never move an inch from being attached to your lotus feet. We are unworthy children of yours. Please guide and keep us attached to your lotus feet birth after birth.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, please forgive us for all the offenses we are committing, knowingly or unknowingly, while trying to execute your mission of spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

We beg to remain your eternal servants,  
Sundara Gopāla dāsa / Ambarīṣa dāsa / Bhakta Utthaman / Bhaktin Chandra Devī

## Soweto

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādī-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our prostrated obeisances again and again in the all-auspicious dust of your divine lotus feet. All glories, all glories, all glories to Śrī Śrīmad Bhaktivedanta Swami Śrīla Prabhupāda, the eternal well-wisher of all living entities!

Because you said to get up before 4:00 a.m. and attend *maṅgala-ārati,* we get up before 4:00 a.m. and attend *maṅgala-ārati.*  
Because you said to chant at least 16 rounds a day, we chant at least 16 rounds a day.  
Because you said to hear *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class, we hear *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.*  
Because you said to eat only *prasādam,* we eat only *prasādam.*  
Because you said that "in Africa, preach to the Africans," we in Africa preach to the Africans.  
Because you said to distribute *prasādam.* we distribute *prasādam.*  
Because you said to scrutinizingly study your books, we scrutinizingly study your books.  
Because you said to follow the GBC, we follow the GBC.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, as we struggle to maintain ourselves firmly on the *vaidhi-bhakti* platform, we long for the day when we shall feel real gratitude for the ineffable kindness you showed to us by teaching us that the real goal of life is not to eat, sleep, mate, and defend in various degrees of sophistication, or to open hospitals, or to write poetry, but to taste the nectar of devotion to the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, in one of the five transcendental mellows.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once said that if you were to reveal your true identity, we would faint. At least we know that you are kṛṣṇa-preṣṭha, very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, and so we have no doubt that if we strictly follow your divine instructions, we will find you at the lotus feet of the Lord. On this most auspicious day of your Vyāsa-pūjā, we humbly beg Your Divine Grace to please give us the benediction that we will always cooperate with our fellow devotees and authorities in ISKCON.

*Your servants in Soweto, South Africa,*

**Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Rūpa-Raghunātha dāsa / Jagat-guru dāsa / Bhakta Mpho / Viśākhā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Maggie / Bhaktin Marisa / Nitāi

## Tegucigalpa

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

Six years ago, when a friend gave us a *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is* to read, we couldn't imagine the revolutionary change that was to come about in our lives. However, now we are amazed at the purifying potency of the Kṛṣṇa consciousness process, for not only have we changed, but also our families.

The world needs mercy—your mercy. We are humbly transmitting it in the form of *prasādam* from our Govinda's Restaurant. It often happens that those to whom we start preaching already know you through your books. In Tegucigalpa's university, they consider Your Divine Grace the greatest philosopher of modem times. But although this is very pleasant to hear, for us you are much more than that—you are their savior and our savior.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for having accepted us in your mission. Thank you for your generous compassion and unmotivated mercy.

*Your eternal servants in Tegucigalpa, Honduras,*

Śyāmasundara dāsa / Mādhava dāsa / Bhakta Lorenzo / Bhakta Lorenzo, Jr.

## Yeoville

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your divine louts feet.

The debt of gratitude the disciple owes the spiritual master can never be repaid, and the more we work hard to repay it, the more we realize we cannot. Our efforts are something like offering a lamp to the sun. But what can we do? We have nothing else to offer.

Purity is the real force in our Kṛṣṇa consciousness. *Māyā* cannot be dodged by the artful use of material intelligence. She cannot be forced out of the way by any display of physical strength. Even if she could, we are too puny to make any impression on her. What do we have to offer? Simply a quagmire of material desires and aspirations and an envious attitude towards the Supreme Lord.

The only way we can make progress spiritually is by taking shelter of your consciousness. The shelter of your purity has enough force to protect any number of sincere souls from *māyā's* clutches. If we abandon our own misconceptions and dreamlike desires and adopt your ideals within our hearts, our lives become transformed—from crow to swan, from dark to light, from demon to devotee.

Within our hearts lurks the ghost of a demon who once ruled our lives. And sometimes he's not just a ghost. Sometimes he tries to come from the subtle level to the gross. See how many he's succeeded in overwhelming, even when they seemed to be at the peak of spiritual life (at least by our standards). He's lurking there in the shadows, waiting for anyone of us who may stray from the shelter of your lotus feet. He hungrily devours any such unfortunate persons, no matter how important or insignificant they may be.

All we may ever have to our credit is an ardent desire to not be swallowed up by Kali. If we fully surrender to your instructions at every moment, trying to help you push on this glorious movement, then we are fully sheltered under the umbrella of your feet, safe from the pernicious effects of the material energy.

Today we pray that we may never leave your shelter. Please help us to carry on in your service under all circumstances. If we stray, please bring us back, for we know there is no place for us in this world other than at your feet.

*Your servants at the Yeoville preaching center, South Africa,*

Raghuvīra dāsa / Raṇacorā dāsa / Kiśora Gopāla dāsa / Bhakta Walter / Bhakta Eddy / Bhakta Rob / Bhakta George / Kuntī-devī dāsī / Arca-vigrahā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Mogandry / Bhaktin Priscilla / Ambarīṣa / Dhruva / Sevānanda
